IV. kaṉṉaṭam



teluṅku moḻikku aṭuttapaṭiyāka iṭam peṟat takkatu kaṉṉaṭam. karnāṭakam eṉṟum, kāṉarīs eṉṟum atu vaḻaṅkap peṟum. micūr, teṉ marāṭṭiya nāṭu, nijām nāṭṭu mēlik kōṭṭaṅkaḷ cila, ākiya pakutikaḷil im moḻi vaḻaṅkivarukiṟatu. malapārk kariyiluḷḷa kāṉarā eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭiyak kōṭṭattil, maliyāḷam, tuḷu, koṅkaṇi ākiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭamum pēcappaṭukiṟatu. nīlakiriyiluḷḷa vaṭakar[1] eṉṉum perun toki makkaḷ pēcuvatu paḻiya kaṉṉaṭamē. im moḻi pēcuvōriṉ mottat toki 1,05,00,000.

karnāṭam allatu karnāṭakam eṉṟa col mutaṟkaṇ teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa iraṇṭu moḻikaḷiyuṅ kuṟippataṟkē potuppaṭa vaḻaṅkap peṟṟatu; piṉṉar, teluṅki nīkkik kaṉṉaṭatti maṭṭumē atu kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. karnāṭakam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiccolliliruntu piṟantatu eṉpar vaṭamoḻip pulavar. āyiṉum, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ kūṟuvatupōl karu+nāṭu+akam eṉṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭiyākap piṟantatu ac col eṉṟu koḷvatē ciṟappākum.

tamiḻiṟ centamiḻ, koṭun tamiḻ eṉṟa iru pirivukaḷ iruppaṉa pōṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum, paḻiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum, putuk kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟum iru pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ip pirivukaḷukkiṭiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu tamiḻiyum maliyāḷattiyum pōṉṟu vaṭamoḻic coṟkalappiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa toṉṟaṉṟu; collākka muṭipukaḷiṉālēyē ēṟpaṭṭa toṉṟākum. paḻiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa moḻiyum, paḻiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa varivaṭivamum oṉṟaṉṟu. atu vēṟu, itu vēṟēyām. iraṇṭiyum oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu kalattal kūṭātu. micūrilum, marāṭṭiya nāṭṭilum kāṇappaṭum kalveṭṭukaḷiṟ pala paḻiya kaṉṉaṭam eṉṉum varivaṭivattā liyaṉṟaṉavēyām. haௗ kaṉṉaṭam eṉṟa paḻiya kaṉṉaṭattiluḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷellām vaṭamoḻiyē yaṉṟik kaṉṉaṭamalla.

karnāṭakam eṉṟa col paṉṉūṟāṇṭukaḷākavē vaḻakkil iruntuvarukiṟatu. ki. pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa varāhamihirar ataṉi eṭuttāḷukiṟār. tāranātarum karnāṭam eṉpatik kuṟikkiṟār. mukammatiyarkaḷ itaṉik karnāṭṭik eṉṟaṉar. āṅkilēyarkaḷ “kāṉarīs” eṉṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkalāyiṉar.



↑ Badagas





V. tuḷu



tuḷu allatu tuḷuvam tiruntiyatoru moḻiyēyākum. eṉiṉum, ataṟkut taṉippaṭṭa varivaṭivamō, ilakkiyamō illāmiyāl ataṉit tiruntiya moḻiyiṉattil cērttal ollumō eṉṟu aiyuṟutal kūṭum. tuḷu moḻiyil mutaṉmutalāka nūlkaḷ cila acciṭṭavarkaḷ pēcil miṣaṉic[1] cērnta kurumārkaḷē yāvar. āṉāl avarkaḷ an nūlkaḷik kaṉṉaṭa moḻiyiṉ varivaṭivilēyē acciṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. ātalāl, tuḷu moḻikkuriya eḻuttu kaṉṉaṭamē eṉṟu nampappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ilakkiyamilliyēṉum tuḷu mikavum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷoṉṟu eṉṟu tāṉ kūṟavēṇṭum.

im moḻi ciṟu tokiyiṉarāṉa makkaḷāl kuṟukiya aḷavuḷḷa oru pakutiyilmaṭṭum pēcappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. kaṉṉaṭak kōṭṭattil oṭum cantirakiri, kalyāṇapuri eṉṟa iraṇṭu āṟukaḷum im moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ iraṇṭu ellikaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vellikaḷik kaṭantu atu vaḻaṅkiyatākak kūṟutaṟku milli.

tuḷu moḻi pēcuvōriṉ toki 5,00,000-kku mēṟpaṭṭatākum. iruntālum avariṭiyē pala moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl tuḷu eṅkē oruvaḻiyāy maṟintoḻintuviṭumō eṉṟukūṭa aiyuṟalām. iruntālum atu maṟintoḻitaṟkāṉa kuṟikaḷ oṉṟum ituvarik kāṇappaṭṭilatu. tirāviṭa makkaḷukkuḷ māṟutal eṉpatē vēṇṭāta ōr iṉattār irukkakkūṭu meṉṟāl avarkaḷ tuḷuvarkaḷē. ātaliṉ avarkaḷ tam moḻiyi maṟintoḻiyavum viṭuvarō!

tuḷu eṉpatu paṇivu, aṭakkam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum eṉa pirikel[2] eṉṟa kaṉṉaṭa ilakkaṇa nūlāciriyar kūṟu kiṟār. itiliruntu accol am moḻi pēcum makkaḷiyē yaṉṟi am moḻiyik kuṟittatākak koḷḷa iṭamilli.

maliyāḷa moḻiyiṉ kiḷi moḻiyē tuḷu eṉṟu ellis[3] nampiṉār. avar avvāṟu nampiyataṟku maliyāḷa moḻi arukiṟ peru vaḻakki liruppatum, tuḷuvap pārppaṉarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuta maliyāḷa eḻuttukkaḷiyē perumpālum payaṉpaṭutti varuvatum kāraṇaṅkaḷākalām. ik kāraṇaṅkaḷ cālā. tuḷuvam maliyāḷattiṉ kiḷiyaṉ ṟeṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟalām. "tuḷu ilakkaṇam" vakutta pirikel itik kuṟittup pala uṇmikaḷiyeṭuttuk kūṟi viḷakkiyuḷḷār. maliyāḷattiṟkum tamiḻukkum irukkum vēṟupāṭṭiviṭa mikunta vēṟupāṭu tuḷuvattiṟkum maliyāḷattiṟku miṭiyē uḷḷatu. āṉāl ataṟkum kaṉṉaṭa moḻikku miṭiyē at tuṇi vēṟupāṭilli. kuṭaku moḻikkum tuḷuvattiṟku miṭiyiluḷḷa vēṟupāṭō mikamikak kuṟivu. tamiḻ moḻiyiliruntō kuṭaku peritum vēṟupaṭukiṟatu.

ceṉṉik karukil vacittuvanta kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa nāṭōṭi iṭiyarkaḷ turattappaṭṭu avarkaḷuṭiya nila pulaṅkaḷ tuḷuva nāṭṭu vēḷāḷarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa eṉṟa oru paḻaṅ katiyi ellis kūṟiyuḷḷār. ceṉṉiyiyum, ceṉṉiyiyaṭuttu muḷḷa cila kuṭumpattiṉar taṅkaḷit tuḷuva vēḷāḷar eṉṟu aḻittukkoḷvatu mēṟkuṟitta paḻaṅ katik koḷkiyi valiyuṟuttuvatākum. iruntālum, tuḷuva nāṭṭiliruntu palar ceṉṉikku vantu kuṭiyēṟi yirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu koḷvataṟkilli ; eṉeṉil, avvāṟiruntāl ceṉṉi nakarat tamiḻ ciṟcila tuṟikaḷilēṉum tuḷuva moḻip paṇpukaḷi ēṟṟukkoṇṭiruttal iyalpākumātaliṉ. avvāṟu tuḷuva moḻik kalappu iruppatākat teriyavilli. ceṉṉi nakarat tamiḻ, ceṉṉi maṇṭilat tamiḻiliruntu vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇpatu teluṅku moḻik kalappiṉālēyē yaṉṟit tuḷumoḻik kalappiṉālaṉṟu.



↑ Basle Missionaries

↑ Mr. Brigel

↑ Mr. Ellis





VI. kuṭaku



kuṭakukku ilakkiyam illi. tuḷuvi oppakkūṭa itaṉip paṇpaṭṭa moḻi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu. eṉavē, mutaṟkaṇ itaṉit taṉi moḻiyākak kaṇakkiṭāmal kaṉṉaṭattuḷ aṭakkik kūṟappaṭṭuvantatu. itaṉ uṟavip paṟṟiya ārāycci iṉṉum teḷivu peṟavilli. ṭākṭar mōkliṅ[1] eṉṟa jermāṉiya ārāycciyāḷar itu kaṉṉaṭattiviṭat tamiḻ maliyāḷam ivaṟṟukku aṇmiyāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. tirāviṭak kuḻuvil iḵtu etaṉuṭaṉ nerukkamāṉa uṟavuṭiya teṉṟu teḷivākat teriyavilli yāyiṉum, mēṟpaṭiyāka nōkkiṉ, itu paḻaṅ kaṉṉaṭattiṟkum tuḷuviṟkum iṭippaṭṭa teṉat tōṟṟukiṟatu. im moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa moṉṟum cila pāṭṭukkaḷum mējar kōl[2] eṉpavarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

“nīlakiriyiluḷḷa paṇṭik kuṭikaḷiṉ moḻikaḷip pōṉṟu im moḻiyum (kuṭaku) taṉ toṉmip paṇpu māṟāmal iruntu varukiṟateṉpatil aiyamilli. iḵtu im makkaḷ perumpālum otuṅki vāḻntu varuvataṉālēyē yākum. kuṭaku makkaḷ paṇṭik kālattilēyē mēṟku malittoṭariṟ kuṭiyēṟiviṭṭaṉar eṉpatu avarkaḷiṭiyē iṉṟum oru peṇ pala āṭavarkaḷi maṇantu koḷḷum vaḻakkamiruntuvaruvataṉāṟ pulappaṭum. iv vaḻakkam paṇṭit tirāviṭar vaḻakkamākum. ivarkaḷukku ilakkiyamilli; pārppaṉar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ iṉṟaḷavum ivarkaḷiṭip pukavilli” eṉṟu parṉel[3] eḻutiyuḷḷār.

itukāṟum viritturitta āṟu moḻikaḷum tirāviṭak kuḻuviṉuḷ tiruntiyaviyākak koḷḷak kiṭappaṉa. iṉik kūṟappōkum āṟum, ivaṟṟiṟku māṟākat, tiruntātaṉa; atāvatu eḻuttum ilakkiyamum illātaṉa.



↑ Dr. Mogling

↑ Major Cole

↑ Burnell's “Specimens of South Indian Dialects: No. 3."





tiruntā moḻikaḷ



(I) tutam :

tutam allatu totam eṉpatu nīlakiri maliyiluṟiyum tutavar allatu totavariṉ moḻiyām. ivarkaḷ toki ekkālattum oṉṟi raṇ ṭāyirattiṟku mikutiyā yiruntirukka muṭiyātu. apiṉip paḻakkattālum, peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavarkaḷik koḷḷum muṟiyālum, peṇ kuḻantikaḷip piṟantavuṭaṉ koṉṟuviṭum vaḻakkam oru kālattil kiyāḷappaṭ ṭiruntamiyālum ivarkaḷ tokiyil aruki, iṉṟu 700 aḷavil kuṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, ivarkaḷ moḻi oppiyal moḻiyilak kaṇattiṟkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟappukkaḷ uṭiyatu.

tuta allatu tota eṉpatu tamiḻt tōḻaṉ (kūṭṭattāṉ) eṉpatuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiyatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar pōp koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, aḵtu oppukkoḷḷat takkataṉṟu.

(II) kōtam :

totavarkaḷē yaṉṟi, nīlakiri maliyil kōtarkaḷ eṉṟa oru ciṟukuṭiyiṉarum vāḻntuvarukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum kūlit toḻilāḷikaḷē yāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ toki ēṟakkuṟiya 1200 ākum. paṇṭi nāṭkaḷilirunta uyarkuṭi makkaḷāl turattappaṭṭu ivarkaḷ iṅkē vantu kuṭiyēṟiyavarka ḷātalvēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ moḻiyākiya kōtam kaṉṉaṭattiṉ koccikkiḷi eṉṟu oruvāṟu kūṟalām. 1911-ām āṇṭu eṭutta makkaṭṭokik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi assām nāṭṭil oṉpatiṉmar kōtar iruntārkaḷām.

iṉi, it totavamum kōtamumēyaṉṟi, vaṭakarkaḷ pēcum oru taṉippaṭṭa moḻiyum, iruḷarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻ moḻic citivum, kuṟumparkaḷ eṉṟa mulli nila makkaḷ pēcum tamiḻmoḻic citivum nīlakiri malit toṭaril vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

(III) kōṇṭu :

kōṇṭarkaḷ mattiya intiyāviluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷilum kāṭukaḷilum uṟiyum paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ āvar. ivarkaḷatu toki patiṉintu nūṟāyiram (15,00,000) ākum.[1] ivarkaḷ vāḻum pakutiyi muṉṉiya ārāycciyāḷar kōṇṭavaṉam eṉṟaḻittaṉar. kōṇṭarkaḷ tāmē tammik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyar kōyītōr[2] atāvatu kōyīkkaḷ eṉpatākum. ivariṭiyē 12 vakuppukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 4 vakuppiṉar tammic ciṟappākak kōyītōr eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. ivaruḷ oru vakuppiṉar kōhitūr eṉṟu tammik kuṟikkum māriyār āvar. ivarkaḷē kōṇṭar aṉivarilum vīramum muraṭṭut taṉamum mikkavar. kalappaṟṟa paḻaṅkōṇṭarkaḷum ivarkaḷē yāvar eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.

(IV) kantam allatu ku :

āṅkila aṟiñar ivarkaḷik kontar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷi aṭuttuḷḷōr ivarkaḷik kantar eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ tāmē tammik ku eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kōṇṭavaṉattiṉ kiḻakkup pakkattilum, orissāviṉ kuṉṟukaḷilum uṟipavarkaḷ. kōṇṭaruṭaṉ uṟavuṭiyavar ivar eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. kōṇṭar eṉṟa peyarum, kantar eṉṟa ivar peyaruṅkūṭak kuṉṟu eṉṟa tamiḻccol allatu ataṟkuc cariyāṉa koṇṭa eṉṟa teluṅkuc colliliruntu vantateṉac cilar collukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, itaṟku ovvāta muṟiyil ivarkaḷi yaṭuttu vāḻum teluṅkarē ivarkaḷik kontar eṉṟum kōtar eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.

kaḻinta (1911) kaṇakkuppaṭi ivarkaḷatu toki aintu nūṟāyirattu muppatiṉāyiram (5,30,000) ākum.

(V) irājmahāl :

mālarkaḷ eṉṟum, irājmahālar eṉṟum ivarkaḷ peyarpeṟuvar. vaṅkāḷakkiluḷḷa irājmahāl malikaḷil ivarkaḷ paṇṭikkālattiliruntu vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ. irājhāl eṉpatu pāṟiyar allatu kuṉṟa makkaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. im moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭi tirāviṭa iṉaktic cērntatē eṉpatu “āciya moḻiyārāyccikaḷ"[3] eṉṟa nūliṉ aintān tokutiyil eṭuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa irājmahāl moḻic coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiliruntum, hāṭjsaṉ eṉpār eḻutiya “vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu"[4] eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟpaṭṭiyiliruntum naṉku viḷaṅkum. cantāḷar eṉ pōriṉ moḻiyōṭu itaṉik kalantuviṭutal kūṭātu. im moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amippippaṟṟi oṉṟun terivataṟkilli. im makkaḷiṉ toki ēṟakkuṟiya 64,000.



↑ kōṇṭarkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ; ākiyāl ikkālattut tiruntiya camūka vāḻkkiyil iṭam peṟak kūṭātavar eṉṟu tāḻtti vikkappaṭṭirunta nilimiyi rīvāp pēraracar nīkki, kōṇṭarkaḷ iṉi kṣattiriyarkaḷākavē pārāṭṭappaṭṭu, kṣattiriyarkaḷukkuriya ellā urimikaḷiyum peṟuvar eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi piṟappittuḷḷār- (asōsi yēṭṭeṭ piras) - intiyaṉ ekspiras (19-2-41)



↑ Koitor

↑ The Asiatic Researches.

↑ Ethnology of Bengal-Col. Dalton.





(VI) ōrāvōṉ :

cūṭiya nākapuriyilum, atic cuṟṟilum vāḻum ōrāvōṉ iṉattāriṉ toki 8,00,000. “vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟu”[1] eṉṟa nūlil karṉal ṭālṭaṉ ivarkaḷip paṟṟiya cuviyāṉa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippoṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivarkaḷ moḻiyum tirāviṭa moḻiyiyē aṭippaṭiyāyuṭiyatākum. pāṭsk pātiriyār im moḻikkuriya ilakkaṇac curukkam[2] oṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.

ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷik kurṅkarkaḷ[3] eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. koṅkaṇattiliruntu vantatākavum, rōtā[4] malikaḷilum pāṭalipurak kōṭṭatti yaṭutta malikaḷilum neṭunāḷ vāḻntiruntatākavum, aṅkiruntu turattappaṭṭa poḻutu irājmahāl malikaḷukku oru pakutiyiṉarum, cūṭiya nākapuri malikaḷukku maṟṟoru pakutiyiṉarumākac ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyatākavum avarkaḷ kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. moḻi yoṟṟumikaḷum, paḻakka vaḻakka voṟṟumikaḷum itaṉi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. mūṇṭarkaḷum kōlēriyarkaḷum vantu kuṭiyēṟuvataṟku muṉṉarē orāvōṉiyarkaḷ cūṭiya nākapuriyil vantuviṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu.

tutam, kōtam, kōṇṭu mutaliya moḻikaḷ tiruttamaṟṟaviyā yiruntapōtilum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiruntiya moḻikaḷip pōlavē avi tūya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉpatil aiyamilli. avaṟṟiṟ kaṭuttapaṭiyāka, irājmahāl, orāvōṉ eṉṟa iraṇṭu moḻikaḷiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷākak koḷḷalām. ēṉeṉil, ivaṟṟil cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ, mutal nāṉku eṇkaḷ pōṉṟa iṉṟiyamiyāc coṟkaḷi uḷḷiṭṭa mutaṟ coṟkaḷil perumpālāṉavi tirāviṭac coṟkaḷē yāyiṉum avaṟṟōṭukūṭap piṟa kuḻuc coṟkaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa. ip piṟakuḻu kōlēriyak kuḻu eṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, ivviru moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷēyāyiṉum piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiviṭat tūymi kuṟintaviyē. ivaṟṟuḷḷum, orāvōṉ irājmahāliyum, kōlēriya iṉattiyum iṇippateṉṟum, irājmahāl atēpōṉṟu orāvōṉiyum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiyum iṇippa teṉṟum hāṭjsaṉ koḷkiṟār. eppaṭiyum ōrāvōṉiviṭa irājmahālē tirāviṭac cārpu mikutiyu muṭiyateṉpatu tēṟṟam.

mattiya maṇṭilattilum, vaṅkāḷattilum vaḻaṅkum tiruntā moḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭilāvatu tirāviṭat toṭarpu irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟip pōntavaṟṟāl iṉitu viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tirāviṭap peruṅ kuḻuviṉar kaṅkik kariyi lillāviṭṭālum, vaṅkāḷa maṇṭilam variyilum paravi yiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu pōtarukiṉṟatu. muṉṉip paḻaṅkālattil tirāviṭak kuḻuviṉar intiyā muḻutumē paraviyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷki oruvāṟu itaṉāl valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.

karṉal ṭālṭaṉ tammuṭiya ”vaṅkāḷa makkaḷ varalāṟu” eṉṟa nūlil tirāviṭat toṭarpik kuṟittuk kūṟiyuḷḷa pakuti varumāṟu:

"potuvāka ippoḻutu nampappaṭṭu varuvatik kāṭṭilum mikap peruvāriyākak tirāviṭat toṭarpu vaṅkāḷa makkaḷiṭi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟu intup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭum vaṅkāḷap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṟ perumpakutiyiṉar tirāviṭakkuḻuvic cērntavarkaḷē. vaṅkāḷak kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum, cūṭiya nākapuri, orisāp pakutikaḷilum avaṟṟi yaṭutta kāṭukaḷilum vāḻntuvarum pūyiyarkaḷ[5] aṉivarum tirāviṭarkaḷē; koccarkaḷ[6] eṉṟa iṉattārum tirāviṭarkaḷē. pūyiyarkaḷiṉ toki ēṟakkuṟiya 25,00,000; koccarkaḷiṉ toki 15,00,000. inta nāṟpatu nūṟāyiravarum vaṅkāḷa maṇṭila makkaṭṭokiyil pattil oru paṅkiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ tirāviṭa iṉattāruḷ cērkkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷē.” im muṭipu ārāyattakka toṉṟākum.

koḷki vēṟṟumik kiṭamuḷḷa ittakiya pakutikaḷi nīkki, aiyattiṟkē iṭamiṉṟit tirāviṭar eṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka vakuppukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tokikaḷum 1911-m āṇṭu makkaḷ tokik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi kīḻē tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ pēcupavar toki

1. tamiḻ 1,91,89,740[7]



2. teluṅku 2,35,42,859



3. kaṉṉaṭam 1,05,25,739



4. maliyāḷam 67,92,277



5. tuḷu 5,31,498



6. kuṭaku 42,881



7. tuṭam 730



8. kōtam 1,280



9. kōṇṭu 15,27,157



10. kantam (ku) 5,30,476



11. irājmahāl 64,875



12. orāvōṉ 8,00,328



mottat tirāviṭar

6,35,49,846



ik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi tirāviṭamoḻi pēcuvōriṉ mottat toki 6 kōṭiyē 40 nūṟāyiram ātal kāṇka. ik kaṇakkil nāṭōṭikaḷāṉa irāmūsikaḷ, ilampāṭikaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷic cērkkavilli. ēṉeṉil, ilampāṭikaḷ pēcuvatu oruvaki intustāṉiyēyākum. irāmūsikaḷ moḻiyō teluṅkiṉ marūu. ātalāl, avi taṉiyākak kūṟappaṭa vēṇṭiyaviyalla. mēṟkuttoṭariṉ teṉ pakutiyil vāḻum maliyaracarkaḷilō caṟṟu vaṭakkil maliyāḷa moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ aṇmiyiluḷḷōr maliyāḷac citivumoḻi oṉṟiyum, teṟkē tamiḻ moḻi vaḻaṅkum pakutiyiṉ aṇmiyiluḷḷōr maliyāḷak kalappuṟṟa tamiḻc citivumoḻi oṉṟiyumē pēcukiṉṟaṉar.

iṉi, mattiya intiyāvilum vaṅkattilum uḷḷa hō, muṇṭā mutaliya kōlēriya moḻikaḷum, cavararkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ cērkkappaṭa villi. ivaṟṟi car jārj kāmpel kōlēriya iṉameṉṟum hāṭjsaṉ tamiḻiṉam eṉṟum vakuttuḷḷārkaḷ. kōṇṭu, ku, irājmahāl, orāvōṉ mutiliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ivi uṟavuṭiyaviyākat tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyē. āṉāl ivvuṟavu orucila tirāviṭa mutaṟcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ niṉṟuviṭukiṟatu. ilakkaṇa amippil yātōr oṟṟumiyumilli. ittakiya uṟavu iṉa uṟavākamāṭṭātu; neṭunāḷiya iṭa aṇmi yiyum paḻakkattiyumē kāṭṭuvatākum.

intiyāviṉ vaṭakiḻakkil pōṭōvar[8] timālar[9] mutaliyavar moḻikaḷiyum, kumāvōṉ[10] asām ivaṟṟiṭiyēyuḷḷa malikkāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiyum ivvāṟē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattiṟ cērkkavilli. hāṭjsaṉ ivaṟṟiyum tamiḻkkuḻu eṉṟē matittaṉar. itaṟkuk kāraṇam avar āriya iṉamallā moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum orē kuḻu eṉṟum, atil talimiyāṉa moḻi tamiḻ eṉṟum koṇṭatē yākum. āṉāl ilakkaṇa amippu, mutaṟcol tokuti ivaṟṟi ārāyntu pārttāl avi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ attakiya nērāṉa iṉa oṟṟumi uṭiyaviyākat tōṟṟa villi. avar kāṭṭiya ciṟcila ilakkaṇa amippu oppumikaḷ im moḻikaḷukkumaṭṭumēyaṉṟic cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum potuppaṭṭaviyām. ātalāl avaṟṟit tirāviṭak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērppatu turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērppati yoppatēyākum eṉka.

palūccistāṉattil kelattut tokutiyil pēcappaṭum pirākuvī moḻi tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟit tirāviṭa ilakkaṇa amippukkūṭa uṭiyatātalāl, atu hāṭjsaṉ tamiḻkkuḻuvil cērtta nēpāḷa, pūtāṉat tamiḻmoḻikaḷiviṭa, tirāviṭak kuḻuvuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭuvataṟku ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku urimiyuṭiya tēyākum. āṉāl atiluṅkūṭat tirāviṭap pakuti kuṟivākavum, piṟa pakuti mikutiyākavum iruppatāl, tirāviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟku utavum iṭaṅkaḷilmaṭṭum aḵtu eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭum.

1911-m āṇṭuk kaṇakkil pirākuvī tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ vittu eṇṇappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ak kaṇakkiṉpaṭi pēcuvōr toki 170,998 ākum. itu tavira 236 pēr pēcum malhar[11] eṉpatoṉṟum, 24,074 pēr pēcum kolāmī[12] eṉpatoṉṟum tirāviṭa iṉattuḷ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pirākuvī moḻiyil orucila talimiyāṉa coṟkaḷ tirāviṭac cārpuṭiyaviyāyiṉum, coṟkaḷuḷ perumpālāṉa tirāviṭattiṉ vēṟāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ilakkaṇa amippilō teḷivāṉa tirāviṭa oṟṟumikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pirāruvī makkaḷ tāṅkaḷ hāleppi[13] (alep pōvi) liruntu vantatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu orukāl avarkaḷiṭiyē piṉvanta oru kūṭṭattārik kuṟippatāyirukka vēṇṭum. aḵtu āriyak kūṭṭamākavum iruntirukkakkūṭum. āṉāl, eppaṭiyum tirāviṭac cārpāṉa oru mutal kūṭṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. atippaṟṟi ittirāviṭa oṟṟumikaḷāl terivatu tavira vēṟetuvum teriyavilli. piṉvanta kūṭṭattār ivarkaḷi veṉṟu ivarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantirukkalām.

pirākuvī moḻi tirāviṭa vakuppic cintu āṟu kaṭantu naṭu āciyāvariyiṟ koṇṭupōyc cērkkiṉṟatu. ammoḻi uṇmiyil pañcāpi, cinti ivaṟṟuṭaṉ otta moḻiyāyiṉum tirāviṭap pakuti oṉṟuṭaṉ iṇintuḷḷa teṉpatu maṟukkakkūṭāta uṇmiyākum. it tirāviṭap pakutiyi nōkkat tirāviṭarum piṉṉāḷiya āriyar, kirēkkar, cittiyar, maṅkōliyar mutaliyavarkaḷippōl vaṭa mēṟkiliruntu vantavartāmō eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamirukkiṉṟatu.



* * *

﻿4. tirāviṭa marapu moḻikaḷ orē mūla moḻiyiṉ maṇṭilavakittiripu moḻikaḷ alla



orē maṇṭilattil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷ kōṭṭattiṟkuk kōṭṭam ciṟcila māṟutalkaḷuṭaṉum, ciṟappiyal pukaḷuṭaṉum vaḻaṅkappaṭutalkūṭum. appoḻutu avaṟṟit ticimoḻikaḷ eṉṟum, vakittiripumoḻikaḷ eṉṟum koḷvatu vaḻakkāṟu. av vaḻakkāṟṟiṉpaṭi mēṟkuṟikkappaṭṭa paṉṉiraṇṭu vaki moḻikaḷiyum orē mūla tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ vakit tiripumoḻikaḷ eṉṟu koḷvataṟkilli. ēṉeṉil, ivaṟṟiṉiṭiyē uḷḷa vēṟṟumi yiyalpukaḷ palappala vātaliṉālēyē. oru vakiyiṉar pēcum moḻiyi maṟṟoruvakiyiṉar eḷitil kēṭṭup poruḷuṇarntukoḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻiyum, maliyāḷattiyum ēṟakkuṟiya neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa vākak koḷḷalām. eṉiṉum, avvam moḻiyil pēcappaṭum eḷiya ciṟu toṭarkaḷiyē maṟṟa moḻiyāḷar uṇarntukoḷḷutal iyalum. aṭukkut toṭarkaḷiyō, cikkalāṉa vākkiyaṅkaḷiyō oruvarukkoruvar tattam moḻiyiṟ kūṟiṉal maṟṟavar aṟintukoḷḷutal iyalātu.

pēcum moḻi orupuṟamirukka, ilakkiyatti eṭuttukkoṇṭālum itē vēṟupāṭutāṉ. tamiḻ, maliyāḷam, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappāṉa ilakkiyak kalivaḷam irukkiṉṟatu. tamiḻ, maliyāḷam, teluṅku ākiya mūṉṟil ovvoṉṟukkum taṉippaṭṭa vari vaṭivamum uḷḷatu. āṉal, ikkāli vaḻaṅkum kaṉṉaṭa eḻuttukkaḷō teluṅkiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu, oruciṟitu māṟupaṭṭiyalukiṉṟaṉa. ik kāraṇattāl kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅkuṭaṉ neruṅkiya uṟavuṭiyatu eṉṟu koḷvataṟku milli. ēṉ? moḻi eṉṟa muṟiyil, kaṉṉaṭa moḻi teluṅkiviṭat tamiḻiyē peritum cārntatākum. atilum, paḻiya kaṉṉaṭa eḻuttukkaḷ teluṅkeḻuttukkaḷukku muṟṟilum vēṟāṉaviyām.

tiruntiya moḻikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭaku eṉṟa āṟaṉuḷ, tamiḻiṟkum teluṅkiṟkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu mikap peritām. iru moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa vērc coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iraṇṭaṟkum potuvākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟami uṇmiyē. eṉiṉum, tamiḻi maṭṭum aṟinta oruvar, teluṅkil ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭi amikkappaṭṭa oru muḻu nīṇṭa coṟṟoṭari aṟintukoḷḷutal eṉpatu eḷitil muṭiyātu; avvāṟē teluṅkimaṭṭum aṟinta oruvar tamiḻil ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭi amikkappaṭṭa oru muḻu nīṇṭa coṟṟoṭari aṟintukoḷḷutal eṉpatum eḷitil muṭiyātu. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē mūla moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kiḷittaṉavē yāyiṉum, iṉṟiya niliyil, oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uṟavuṭiya taṉippaṭṭa moḻikaḷākavē karutappaṭum.

tiruntiya im moḻikaḷiṉiṭiyē ittuṇi vēṟu pāṭāṉal, tiruntā moḻikaḷāṉa tutam, kōtam, kōṇṭu, kantam, orāvōṉ eṉṟa moḻikaḷukkiṭiyē mikuntu kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumikaḷi eṭutturikkavum vēṇṭumō? uṇmiyik kūṟiṉ, avaṟṟiṟ kiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumi mikutiyāl, avi oṉṟuṭa ṉeṉṟu vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatic cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ eṭuttu viḷakkik kāṭṭutaliviṭṭu, aviyellām orē mūla moḻiyic cārntaviyē eṉṟum, am mūla moḻi tirāviṭa moḻitāṉ eṉṟum cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ īṇṭu viḷakkippōtalē, kaṭamiyāyiṟṟu.





tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vēṟāṉavi:

taṅkaḷukkut terinta ovvoṉṟum pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭatākattā ṉirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu vaṭamoḻip paṇṭitarkaḷ iyalpākavē nampivantārkaḷ. ataṉi yoṭṭiyē avarkaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, vaṭa intiya moḻi marapukaḷiliruntu ettuṇiyō māṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, vaṭamoḻi yiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavikaḷē eṉṟu cātittuvantaṉar. itaṉip paṇṭiya airōppiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷum eḷitil nampivantaṉar. avarkaḷ ārāynta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum ōraḷaviṟku vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ tatpavamākavum, tatcamamākavum kalantirukkak kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, am moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻik kalappillāta coṟkaḷum, marapumoḻikaḷum pala iruntaṉa veṉpatiyum, aviyē ammoḻikaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ eṉpatiyum, avaṟṟilētāṉ avvam moḻikaḷiṉ taṉippaṭṭa uyirnili amintukiṭantatu eṉpatiyum avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintukoḷḷavilli. ataṉ payaṉāka, avarkaḷ ammoḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyic cārntaṉavallāta pakutika ḷellām yātō oru paṇṭiya puṟanāṭṭu moḻiyic cārntavi eṉṟu kūṟivantaṉar. ak koḷkippaṭi tirāvirarkaḷukkum,[1] keḷararkaḷukku[2]miṭiyē mutaṉmiyāṉa vēṟupāṭukaḷ kiṭiyā. ataṟkukkāraṇam, vaṅkāḷi mutaliya keḷariya iṉattic cērnta moḻika ḷellām vaṭamoḻic citivukaḷēyāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻi yallāta coṟkaḷum, marapumoḻikaḷum oru cilavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. avaṟṟip pōṉṟaviyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa coṟkaḷum, marapu moḻikaḷum eṉṟu avarkaḷ ūkittami iyalpē. eṉavē, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntutāṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭik kokkarittu vantārkaḷ kōlpuruk, kāri, vilkiṉs mutaliya airōppiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi yārāycciyil vallunarēyāyiṉum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip paṟṟiya variyil mikavuṅ kuṟinta aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷeṉṟē, avaṟṟippaṟṟi oṉṟumē teriyātavarkaḷeṉṟētāṉ koḷḷalvēṇṭum. oppilakkaṇattiṉ uṇmikaḷit terinta evarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amitikaḷiyum, coṟṟokutikaḷiyum, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amitikaḷōṭum, coṟṟokutikaḷōṭum oppiṭṭup pārttapiṉ, avi ekkāraṇattik koṇṭum oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu uṟavuṭiyaṉa veṉṟō, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntutāṉ tirāviṭa moḻi ilakkaṇa amippum, collākkat tiripukaḷum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟō kūṟa muṉvaramāṭṭār eṉpatu tiṇṇam.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum vaṭamoḻikku maṭṭumēyaṉṟi, av viraṇṭiṟkum intu-airōppiya[3] moḻikaḷukkumē ētō oruvakiyāṉa mūlat toṭarpu iruntirukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu koḷvatō, ak koḷkiyē aṭippaṭiyāka, intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattil tirā viṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭampeṟa urimi yuṇṭu eṉṟu koḷvatō, vaṭamoḻiyi liruntē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkikku muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkikaḷākum. ilakkaṇa amippu muṟikaḷiyum coṟ ṟokutikaḷiyum ārāyntāl, mēṟ kuṟitta intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu niṟuvutal oruvāṟu iyalum. āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē cittiya[4] moḻiyiṉattiliruntu intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ pirintuviṭṭaṉa. im muṭipu piṉṉiṣ[5] moḻiyilum, turukkiya moḻiyilum kāṇappaṭum cila paṇṭi mūlac coṟkaḷāl valiyuṟukiṟatu; atupōlavē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum āriyar varukikku muṟpaṭṭa kālattu vaḻaṅkiyiruntaṉavākak karutakkūṭiya cila moḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟami mēṟkūṟiya muṭipi valiyuṟuttuvatākum. ataṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ic coṟkaḷi vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu peṟṟaṉa eṉṟē, aṉṟi iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkum potuvāṉa oru paṇṭip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟṟirukkakkūṭum eṉṟē koḷvatu tavaṟeṉpatum, arukil vaḻaṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi liruntu vaṭamoḻi pala coṟkaḷip peṟṟup payaṉpaṭuttap piṉvāṅkavilli yeṉṟu koḷvatē cariyāṉa muṭipeṉpatum, eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum.

eḵtu eppaṭiyāyiṉum, vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ piṟantirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu koṇṭa paḻaṅ koḷki kuruṭṭuk koḷkiyē eṉpatil aiyamēyilli. inti, vaṅkāḷi mutaliya keḷariya moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu tōṉṟiyirukkakkūṭu mātalāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum avvāṟētāṉ tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷutal cālpāmō? ākātu. kīḻvarum uṇmikaḷic carivā uyttuṇarāmiyē tavaṟāṉa im muṭipukkuk kāraṇamākum.

(1) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil vaṭamoḻic cārpillāta taṉip pakuti, vaṭamoḻic cārpuḷḷa pakutiyiṉum, mikamika virintatoṉṟu eṉpatip paṇṭiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭaṉar.

(2) oru moḻiyiṉ uyirnilikaḷ eṉṟu karutap paṭuvaṉavāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, viṉittiripukaḷ, peyart tiripukaḷ, collākka muṟi mutaliya ellāvaṟṟilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu aṭiyōṭu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa kaṇkūṭāṉa uṇmiyiyum avarkaḷ puṟakkaṇittuviṭṭārkaḷ.

(3) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉavē eṉṟu koṇṭa mēṉāṭṭārāycciyāḷar taṅ koḷkikkut tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil akkāli veḷiyiṭappaṭṭirunta akara varicikaḷiyē[6] ātāramākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa māṟutaliṉṟi appaṭiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa ; māṟutal peṟṟa cila coṟkaḷ tam vaṭamoḻit toṭarpi naṉku terivippaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻip pulavarkaḷ ic coṟkaḷit tirāviṭac coṟkaḷākak koḷḷāmal, (vaṭamoḻiyiliruntu vanta) ticic coṟkaḷākavē koṇṭaṉar eṉpatum, avaṟṟiṉ tiripaḷaviṟkēṟṟavāṟu avi avarkaḷāl oḻuṅkupaṭuttip patippikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum pāvam! av vārāycciyāḷarkkut teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa coṟkaḷiṉ tokutiyē perum pakutiyākum eṉpati uṇarntu, ac coṟkaḷit taṉiyākap pirittu muṟippaṭutti nāṭṭu moḻic coṟkaḷ allatu “ceñcoṟkaḷ” eṉṟu pārāṭṭivantaṉar eṉpati av vārāycciyāḷar yāṅkaṉam aṟintiruppar?

uṇmiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷiyum, citivukaḷiyum pirittaṟital aritaṉṟu. orucila coṟkaḷē vaṭa collā, tirāviṭac collā eṉṟu ārāyntaṟitaṟ kuriyaṉavām. nīr eṉpatum mīṉ eṉpatum iru moḻiyāḷarālum tattam moḻiccoṟkaḷ eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, iraṇṭum tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ eṉpatē ēṟpuṭittākum.





↑ Draviras

↑ Gauras

↑ Indo-European

↑ Scythian

↑ Finnish

↑ 1. Dictionaries.





(ovvoru moḻiyilum neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarkaḷiyum, uṭaluṟuppukkaḷiyum, eṉṟum māṟātaṉavum potuppaṭiyāṉaviyumāṉa iravu, pakal, ñāyiṟu, maram, kal, vīṭu, yāṉi, kutiri pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ avvam moḻiyiṉ mutaṉmiyāṉa coṟkaḷākak karutappaṭum. iṭap peyarkaḷum eṇṇup peyarkaḷum māṟupaṭutal iyalpākalāṉ avaṟṟi mutaṉmic coṟkaḷōṭu īṇṭuc cērppataṟkilli. piṉvarum paṭṭiyil, oppunōkki uyattaṟitaṟ keḷitāka vaṭa moḻiyiliruntum, tamiḻiliruntum 60 mutaṉmic coṟkaḷ tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.) ﻿(iṭappeyarkaḷ eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ nīṅkalāka) vaṭamoḻi, tamiḻmoḻikaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa 60 mutaṉmic coṟkaḷ aṭaṅkiya paṭṭi.

kol इन haṉ

(4) mēlum, vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu tōṉṟiyaṉavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉṟu nampiya mēṉāṭṭārāyc ciyāḷarkaḷukku, vaṭa coṟkaḷiyē eṭuttāḷāmalō, aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟumāka mikavum arukiya niliyilmaṭṭum eṭuttāṇṭō varum tiruntā moḻikaḷum tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattil irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē teriyātu. aṉṟiyum, vaṭacoṟkaḷi ōraḷaviṟku eṭuttāḷum tiruntiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum kaṭṭāyamāka avaṟṟi eṭuttāḷattāṉ vēṇṭumeṉpatillāmal, viruppamuḷatēṟ payaṉpaṭuttiyum, iṉṟēl aṟavē yotukkiyum varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum avarkaḷukkut teriyātu. āṉāl, iṉṟiya niliyi nōkkiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyi aṟavē nīkkuva teṉpatu teluṅku moḻikku aritākum; kaṉṉaṭa moḻikkō mikavum aritu; maliyāḷa moḻikkō aritiṉumaritu. im moḻikaḷ kaṇakku vaḻakkillāmal vaṭa coṟkaḷi eṭuttāṇṭuvantuḷḷamiyālum, ac coṟkaḷiṉ utaviyi nāṭuvatē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷamiyālum, tattam ciṟappup paṇpukaḷi yiḻantu, taṉittu niṉṟiyaṅkum āṟṟaliyum iḻantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ aṉittiṉum mikavum tiruntiya paṇpaṭṭa niliyiluḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyō, vēṇṭumeṉṟāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷi aṟavē akaṟṟit taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟal vāyntiruppatōṭaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ utavi yillāmal mikavum mēmpaṭṭu, vaḷamuṟṟu miḷirum āṟṟalum vāyntatākum. paṇṭit tūya tamiḻmoḻikkuc centamiḻ eṉṟu peyar. ic centamiḻilēyē tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṇṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟkaḷō mikamikak kuṟivu. iṉṟiya pēccut tamiḻuṭa ṉum, urinaṭit tamiḻuṭaṉum innili māṟupaṭṭatoṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum. etaṉāl avvāṟeṉilō, kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy iruntu vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum, vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukkaḷiyum vilakki, tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ, marapumoḻikaḷ, amippukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiyē kiyāṇṭu vantuḷḷamiyiṉālē tāṉ. tamiḻmakkaḷiṭiyē ic centamiḻ moḻip pātukāppu evvaḷavukkup paraviyirunta teṉpatu, eḻutappaṭṭa oru tamiḻ nūlil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa coṟkaḷ arukik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō, avvaḷavukku avvaḷavu atu ciṟantatoru nūleṉṟum, evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu atu tāḻntatoru nūleṉṟum karuti matippiṭum paḻakkam paṇṭutoṭṭup payiṉṟu varukiṉṟamiyiṉālēyē iṉitu teḷiyappaṭum. piṟa moḻi nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟil evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu vaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu an nūlkaḷ avvammoḻiyiṉarāṟ ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum; tamiḻilō evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ utaviyi nāṭāmal taṉittiyaṅku kiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu ciṟappuṭaṉ pōṟṟappaṭum. uṇṇāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷilum vāḻntuvarum tāḻnta makkaḷiṭiyē vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷip pēccuvaḻakkilum kiyāḷāmal otukkum tūya paḻakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyiṉ toṉmic ciṟappunili am moḻiyiliyalum ceyyuḷkaḷilum, tāḻnta kuṭimakkaḷiṉ pēccukaḷilumiruntē ārāyntu kāṇappaṭum eṉpatu oru potu uṇmiyākum. piṟ kālattil mikavum valintu muyaṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭa tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷilum, pārppaṉarkaḷ pēcum tamiḻilum, mikavum kaṟṟavarkaḷākak karutappaṭum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ pēccilumē vaṭamoḻi mikipaṭap pāvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atuvum, camaya uṇmi, aṟiviyal, tattuvam, eṉak kalikaḷiluḷḷa marapuc coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi viḷakkumiṭaṅ kaḷilmaṭṭumē ivvāṟu vaṭacoṟkaḷ perumpāṉmiyum kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷiṉ toki, āṅkila nūlkaḷiṟ kiyāḷappaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭum ilattiṉ moḻic coṟkaḷiṉ tokiyi viṭa mikutiyāṉatoṉṟaṉṟu.

eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila viviliya nūlilum, tamiḻ viviliya nūlilum ”pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa pakutiyi oppumikkāka eṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ col tokutikaḷi ārāyvōm. āṅkilac coṟkaḷi ārāyu miṭattu nēriṭiyāṉa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷiyum, nār maṉ pireñcu mūlamāka vanta ilattīṉ citivukaḷiyum oṉṟākac cērttu ilattīṉākak kaṇakkiṭuvōm. ataṉ palaṉ kīḻvarum paṭṭikippaṭi yākum.

col vaki ﻿āṅkilam ﻿tamiḻ

mottam ilattīṉ tūya āṅkilam mottam vaṭamoḻi tamiḻ

peyarum peyaruriyum 43 14 29 53 21 32

viṉi 20 7 13 34 7 27

eṇṇup peyar 5 0 5 6 1 5

”āyiram” eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar. ataṉālēyē eṇṇup peyari ṉetiril vaṭamoḻiyiṉ kīḻ ’1’ eṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. mottamākak kaṇakkiṭṭāl āṅkilattil varum ilattīṉum, tamiḻil varum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṭittāka nūṟṟukku 45 viḻukkāṭu ākiṟatu. iraṇṭilum iṭap peyarkaḷ, uriccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭiccoṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa uṟuppukkaḷ ivi muṟṟilum tāymoḻiyic cērntaviyēyākum.

āṅkilamoḻi taṉ vaḷarccimuṟiyil tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉ moḻiyi liruntum, ilattīṉ moḻiyiliruntum evvakiyil utavipeṟṟirukkiṟatu eṉpati āṅkila camayat talivar ṭreñc[1] piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār:-

“im moḻiyi (āṅkilam)ṉuṭiya iṇippukaḷ, oli muṟi, nāṭi narampukaḷ, taci nārkaḷ, iṭaccoṟkaḷ, urupukaḷ, iṭiccoṟkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, tuṇi viṉikaḷ, yāppu muṟikkup payaṉpaṭum ciṟuciṟu acic coṟkaḷ, ilakkaṇa muṟiyamippu ākiyavi ellām taṉippaṭṭa āṅkilō cāksaṉē. it teyvikak kaṭṭiṭattiṟku ilattīṉ moḻi utaviyavi ceṅkaṟkaḷum, naṉku cetukkik kaṭica liṭappaṭṭa calavik kaṟkaḷumē yām; āṉāl ivi yellāvaṟṟiyum patittup piṇittuk kaṭṭiṭamāka eḻuppuvikka utaviya nīṟu, cuṇṇāmpu, cāntu ellām tūya āṅkilō cāksaṉē’’

ikkūṟṟu appaṭiyē tirāviṭattiṟkum vaṭamoḻikkum poruntuvatākum; mēṟ pakutiyil “āṅkilō cāksaṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷi lellām, “tirāviṭam” eṉpatiyum, ”ilattīṉ” eṉṟu varumiṭaṅkaḷilellām ”vaṭamoḻi” eṉpatiyum amittukkoṇṭu paṭittāl tirāviṭa moḻikkum vaṭamoḻikkum iṭiyē et tuṇi yuṟavu irukkiṟa teṉpatu teḷḷat teḷiya viḷaṅkum.

mēṟ “pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa tamiḻ viviliya nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟṟokiyum āṅkila nūṟpakutiyil kāṇappaṭum ilattīṉ coṟṟokiyum viyakkattakka vaṇṇam oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉ pati aṟintōm. ataṉāl āṅkilam ilattīṉukku evvaḷaviṟkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatō, avvaḷaviṟkut tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkuk kaṭaṉpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu tavaṟākum. āṅkilap pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa ilattīṉ coṟkaḷi nīkkiviṭa muyaṉṟāl, ataṉ poruḷ nuṭpaṅ keṭṭuviṭum. āṉāl, tamiḻp pakuti taṉmāṭṭuḷḷa vaṭa coṟkaḷil perumpakutiyiyō, aṉṟi muḻuvatiyumō nīkkiviṭṭut takka tamiḻc coṟkaḷip peytukoṇṭāl evvakiyiluṅ kuṟivuṟātu; avvāṟu nīkkuvatāl ataṉ tūymi māṟṟum, cemmip paṇpum uyarntoḷirumē yaṉṟik kuṟintu maṅkā. ilattīṉiliruntu peṟṟa coṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟiṟku āṅkilō cāksaṉil nērc coṟkaḷ kiṭiyā; eṉavē, ac coṟkaḷi nīkkiviṭuva teṉṟāl avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷi nīṇṭa, aḻakaṟṟa coṟṟoṭar kaḷāltāṉ viḷakkavēṇṭum. āṉāl tamiḻō, taṉippaṭṭa vakiyil colvaḷam niṟintatu. appaṭiyirukka aḵtēṉ vaṭacoṟkaḷi eṭuttāṇṭa teṉṟāl, colvaḷa miṉmiyāṉaṉṟu; putumi karutiyēyām eṉṟu viṭukka. ”pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷ” eṉṟa viviliya nūṟpakutiyi nāṭṭuppuṟat tāḻnta makkaḷiṉ pēccu muṟiyil amittāl, atiluḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ toki mikavuṅ kuṟintu viṭum. ataṉiyē cemmippaṭuttit tirutti ilakkiya naṭiyil amippōmāṉāl, atil eñciyuḷḷa oṉ ṟiraṇṭu vaṭacoṟkaḷum māyamāyp pōyviṭum. appakutiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟiṟkuttāṉ kuṟṟañ collāta vakiyil nērt tamiḻccol amikka muṭiyātu. ac col ”vikkirakam”[2] eṉpatu ac collum ataṉ karuttum tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum, koḷkikaḷukkum puṟampāṉavi. pārppaṉarkaḷāl purāṇak karuttukaḷōṭum, ”vikkiraka ārātaṉi muṟi” eṉṉum uruva vaḻipāṭṭōṭum, ic collum karuttum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉuṭ pukuttap paṭṭaṉavākum. pārppaṉarkaḷiṉ camayattiṟku in nāṭṭil mutalil irunta celvākkiṉālēyē, camayak karuttukkaḷit terivippaṉavāyt tamiḻ nūṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷākavō, ac coṟ citivukaḷākavō kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṟṟa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ illi eṉpatilli; muṟṟilum ēṟṟaviyum, orōvaḻi vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉum ciṟantaṉavākavuṅ koḷḷak kūṭiya coṟkaḷ uḷa. avi yellām, iṉṟu vaḻak kiḻantu, maṟintu, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilmaṭṭum aruki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kalvi maṅkiyuḷḷa innāḷil, uri naṭiyil ac coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkumēl an naṭi mikavum karaṭumuraṭāṉa teṉṟum, kaṭapaṭām pōṉṟa teṉṟum karuti ikaḻappaṭum. tamiḻc camaya nūlkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ potuvāka eṭuttāḷappaṭ ṭuḷḷamikku ituvē, uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇamum, orē kāraṇamumākum.

ēṉiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil epporuḷik kuṟitteḻunta kaṭṭuriyāyiṉuñ cari, ceyyuḷāyiṉuñ cari, ataṉkaṇ mikutiyāṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ payiṉṟirukkak kāṇalām. avi putumi karuti eṭuttāḷappaṭṭaṉavalla; kiṭṭattaṭṭa iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākavē karutik kiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. inta nilimi vantataṟkuk kāraṇam am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiya vaḷarcci mutaṉmiyākap pārppaṉarkaḷ kiyilēyē oppaṭikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamiyēyām. teluṅku moḻiyi yeṭuttukkoṇṭāl, am moḻiyil ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eḻutiyavarkaḷuḷḷum mutaṉmiyāṉavarkaḷum, peyar pōṉavarkaḷum pārppaṉarkaḷē. am moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ pārppaṉa rallātārāṉa āciriyaroruvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākum. uṇmiyākap pārttāl, teluṅkumakkaḷ eṉṟu aḻippataṟku evvakiyilum uriyārāṉa teluṅkup pārppaṉa rallāta tokutiyiṉar taṅkaḷ moḻivaḷarcci, kalivaḷarcci ākiya vaṟṟip pārppaṉarkaḷiṭamē oppaṭittuviṭṭavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.

tamiḻilō ippaṭi yilli. nūl eṉṟa peyarukkuriya ṉavāyp pōṟṟic cēmittuvikkavēṇṭiya vakiyil pārppaṉarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉavāyk kāṇakkūṭiyavi mikamikac cilavē. tamiḻmakkaḷāl avarkaḷuṭiya tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻiṉ vaḷarcciyum, kaliyum, nākarikamum paṇṭutoṭṭuk kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāy ārvattuṭaṉum veṟṟiyuṭaṉum pōṟṟappaṭṭuvarukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya āciriya variciyil pārppaṉarkaḷ eytiyatellām uriyāciriyar eṉṟa nilikku mēṟpaṭṭatilli. tāḻnta vakuppiṉarāya tiruvaḷḷuvārāl eḻutappaṭṭa tirukkuṟaḷukkup pārppaṉārāṉa parimēlaḻakar eṉpār vakuttuḷḷa uriyē mikavuñ ciṟantatoṉṟākap pārāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.

teṉpakutiyil pēcappaṭum moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyip piṉpaṟṟiyē amikkappaṭṭapōtilum, taṉip paṭṭa ilakkiya vaḷatti ēṟpaṭuttikkoḷḷa avaṟṟuṭ cila virumpi muyaṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉa ṭam, maliyāḷam ākiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mutaṉmi yāṉa ilakkiyaṅkaḷellām vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppō, aṉṟi virivuriyō ātalvēṇṭum eṉṟum, av virivuri yeḻutuvatilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarkaḷ eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ[3] eṉpavar kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻippaṟṟiya variyil ik kūṟṟu oppattakkataṉṟu. tamiḻil iraṇṭu oppaṟṟa ciṟanta nūlkaḷāka ulakattōrāl karutappaṭuvaṉa kuṟaḷum, cintāmaṇiyumām. iv viraṇṭum muṟṟilum vaṭamoḻit toṭarpaṟṟavi. tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlamip piyum naṭiyiyum piṉpaṟṟiyirukkakkūṭum; toṭarukkut toṭar moḻipeyarttuḷḷaṉar eṉpati oppuk koḷḷa muṭiyātu. tamiḻ irāmāyaṇam, māpāratam pōṉṟa kāviyaṅkaḷ ivvāṟu eḻuntaṉavēyām. tamiḻar taṅkaḷ kamparāmāyaṇam mutaṉūlākiya vālmīki irāmāyaṇattiṉum mikavuñ ciṟantatu eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭuvatē itaṟkuc cāṉṟākum.

(5) moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟaṉiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumi vēṟṟumikaḷi viḷakkap peritum payaṉpaṭum muṟikaḷuḷ mikavum muṭivāṉatu am moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippi oppiṭṭuc cīrtūkkip pārppatē yām. am muṟippaṭi tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻi yiṉum vēṟāṉavi yeṉṟum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇippativiṭa, cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu iṇippatē cālpuṭittākum eṉṟum tuṇintu kūṟalām.



↑ Archbishop Trench

↑ Image

↑ Professor Wilson





﻿5. ilakkaṇa amippil vaṭamoḻikkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ



(i) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil uyiraṟṟa poruḷkaḷ maṭṭu maṉṟi, pakuttaṟivaṟṟa uyirkaḷum potuppāl (aḵṟiṇi) ākavē matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṇpāl, peṇpāl vakuppu, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ, atilum paṭarkkiyilmaṭṭumē, kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. peyaruriccoṟkaḷilum, paṭarkki viṉikaḷilum ip paṭarkkip peyariṉ tiripē vikutiyāy niṉṟu pāl uṇarttukiṉṟatu. uyirvakikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ pālvaki kāṭṭavēṇṭiṉ āṇ peṇ eṉṟa peyarkaḷic cērttē yākavēṇṭum; appōtum avi āṇ allatu peṇ pālāvatilli; potuppālēyākum. ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟik kuṟikkum iṭappeyarkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ payaṉiliyāṉa viṉikaḷum potuppāl (aḵṟiṇi) muṭiviyē koḷḷalvēṇṭum. ittakiya pālvakuppu vaṭamoḻi mutaliya intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ pakaṭṭāṉa puṉiviyal pālpākupāṭṭiṉiṉṟum muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu. āṉāl cittiyak kuḻuviṉ vaḻakkukku iḵtu ottatu.

(ii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil peyarc coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumi ēṟkumpōtu vikutiyēṟṟu urumāṟuva tilli; ac coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu eḷitil pirikkakkūṭiya piṉ aṭikaḷ allatu urupukaḷiyē ēṟṟu māṟukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum paṉmip peyariṉ vēṟṟumi yuruvāṉatu urupukaḷi ēṟku muṉ paṉmi vikuti peṟuvatoṉṟit tavira maṟṟev vakiyilum orumi vēṟṟumi yuruviliruntu māṟupaṭuvatilli; cittiya moḻikaḷum ivvāṟē.[1]

(iii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷukkup paṉmi perumpālum vaḻaṅkuvatilli; viṉic collil iḵtu iṉṉum arumi.

(iv) tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ nāṉkām vēṟṟumi yurupākiya ku-ki-allatu ke vaṭamoḻi allatu intu - airōppiya (āriya) moḻikaḷiluḷḷa enta vēṟṟumi vikutiyuṭaṉum ottatāka illi. āṉāl atē camayattil atu kīḻit turukkiyuṭaṉum[2], pehistaṉ[3] paṭṭayaṅkaḷuṭaṉum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉum toṭar puṭiyatāyirukkiṉṟatu.

(v) intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷil muṉ ṉurupukaḷ[4] allatu oṭṭukkaḷ (pirattiyayam) payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiyak kuḻu moḻikaḷum piṉṉurupukaḷi allatu oṭṭukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. iv urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum ilakkaṇa muṟippaṭi taṉiccolvakikaḷ alla, peyarccol tiripukaḷēyām. ivvāṟē avaṟṟiṉ viṉi uriccoṟkaḷum peyar allatu toḻiṟ peyar allatu viṉi yeccaṅkaḷē yākum. avi eppōtum tām taḻuvum viṉikaḷukku muntiyē varukiṉṟaṉa.

(vi) vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum peyaruric coṟkaḷ peyarc coṟkaḷip pōlavē pāl, eṇ, iṭam ēṟkiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum avi pāl mutaliyavi ēṟpa tilli. ip peyaruric coṟkaḷ uṇmiyil paṇpup peyarkaḷi liruntu vantaviyē yākum. paṇpup peyar eṉṟu taṉippaṭa niṟkumpōtu, avi piṟa peyarkaḷ pōlavē tiṇi pāl mutaliyavi ēṟpiṉum, piṟa peyarka ḷuṭaṉ oṭṭappeṟṟu uriccollākap payaṉpaṭumpōtu aṅṅaṉam eṟpatilli.

(vii) iṉṉeru vakiyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ maṅkōliyam, mañcu mutalāṉa cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ottum intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭṭum uḷḷaṉa. aḵtāvatu, iyalumpō tellām im moḻikaḷ peyaruric colli vaḻaṅkāmal, viṉiccolliṉ peyarecca urupiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. (uyar maram - eṉpataṟku uyarnta maram eṉpatupōla eṉka.) ivvaḻakkattiṉ payaṉāyp paṇpup peyarkaḷip peyaruḷiyāka vaḻaṅkumpōtu kūṭa, ataṉuṭaṉ peyaruriccol vikutiyāka ’āṉa’ eṉṟa peyareccam cērttukkoḷḷappaṭu kiṟatu. [civappāṉa.]

(viii) taṉmip paṉmiyil muṉṉiliyi uṭpaṭuttiyum uṭpaṭuttāmalum iraṇṭu vakikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikkum,cittiya moḻikaḷukkumē ciṟappāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭa moḻiyilum, intu - airōppiya moḻiyilum iṅṅaṉam illi. itaṉi yoppatāy oruvāṟu kuṟikkakkūṭiyatu piṟkuṟitta moḻikaḷiluḷḷa irumi[5] eṉṟa eṇṇēyākum.

(ix) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ceyappāṭṭu viṉikkut taṉi vaṭivam kiṭiyātu. paṭu eṉṉum tuṇiviṉi cērkkappaṭṭē ap poruḷil vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṟatu. (x) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum, cittiya moḻikaḷum intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷip pōla iṇippu iṭiccoṟkaḷip[6] payaṉpaṭuttāmal "ceytu" eṉṟa viṉiyeccattiyē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

(xi) uṭaṉpāṭu, etirmaṟi eṉṟa iv viāṇṭu viṉivaṭivaṅka ḷiruppatu tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkum, vaṭamoḻikkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭum taliyāya vēṟṟumikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukku uḷḷa oppumikaḷil itu talimiyāṉatākak kāṇkiṟatu.

(xii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, maṅkōliyam mañcu, mutaliyavi, ōraḷavukkup piṟa cittiya moḻikaḷ ākiya ivaṟṟiṉ taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappup paṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu iṇippu iṭappeyarkaḷiṉiṭamākap peyareccaṅkaḷ[7] vaḻaṅkuvatām. ip peyareccam mukkālaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa viṉiyeccat tuṭaṉ āṟām vēṟṟumi urupōṭotta a-eṉṉum cāriyi peṟṟu vantatu ākum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ etaṉilum iṇippu iṭappeyar eṉpataṉ niḻalkūṭak kāṇappaṭavilli. kōṇṭu moḻiyil maṭṭum viṉiyec caṅkaḷ illāmal, oḻintupōṉati oṭṭi, hiṅki moḻiyiṉ iṇippu iṭappeyar kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.

(xiii) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, cittiya moḻikaḷilum taḻuvu moḻikaḷum, taḻuvu toṭarkaḷum taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉṉatākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. intu - airōppiya moḻi iṉattil avi potuvāka muṉvarukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, im moḻikaḷ aṉittilum vākkiyattiṉ mutalil eḻuvāyum, muṭivil payaṉiliyum, eḻuvāy aṭi[8] eḻuvāykku muṉṉum, iṭiyil ceyappaṭuporuḷum viṉi aṭikaḷum ākavarukiṉṟaṉa; peyaraṭikaḷum peya reccaṅkaḷum peyariṉ āṟām vēṟṟumiyum peyarukku muṉṉum, iṭa urupukaḷum kāla urupukaḷum peyarukku muṉ aṉṟik tām taḻuvum peyarkaḷukkum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi urupukaḷukkum piṉṉākavum varukiṉṟaṉa. viṉi yeccaṅkaḷum viṉi aṭikaḷ pōla viṉikku muṉṉākiṉṟaṉa. ellāvaṟṟiyum taḻuvum viṉimuṟṟu iṟutiyilēyē niṟkiṟatu. ivvāṟu collamippu, coṟṟoṭaramippu muṟiyilum cittiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa.

mēṟkūṟiya ilakkaṇa amitikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ potuvāka intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭē iruppatum, neṭunāḷ kalantu vāḻntuṅkūṭa vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum iṉṉum vēṟupaṭṭēyiruppatum kāṇka. atē camayattil, eṅkeṅkō naṭu āciyāvilum, vaṭa āciyāvilum citaṟik kiṭakkum cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avi porunti iruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟu, vaṭamoḻi vēṟu eṉpatu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl naṉku teriyappaṭum. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu peṟappaṭṭaṉa alla; aṉṟi ataṉuṭaṉ cērkkappaṭakkūṭiya paṇpuṭiyaṉavu malla.

cila kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil vaṭamoḻi piṟa intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷiviṭac cittiya moḻik kuḻuvuṭaṉ uṟavuṭiyatāyt tōṟṟuvatu oru vēḷip paḻaṅkuṭikaḷuṭaṉ uḷḷa neṭunāṭ paḻakkattip poṟuttatā yirukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyil (ṭa ṇa ṣa) eṉṟa nāaṭi aṇṇa meykaḷ iruppaṉavākum. iv vaki yil, ”moḻi yiyalil cīṉattiṉ iṭam”[9] eṉṟa nūlil eṭkiṉs eṉpār koṇṭa putiya koḷkiyik kuṟippiṭal vēṇṭum. urupiṉṟi vikutiyilēyē peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi amital, col loḻuṅku, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talivākkiyattiṟku muṉvaral, viṉimuṟṟu iṟutiyil niṟṟal ākiya ilakkaṇa amippukkaḷuḷ vaṭamoḻi āriya iṉattiviṭat turāṉiya iṉattiyē piṉpaṟṟi yuḷḷatu eṉa avar kūṟukiṟār. āṉāl vaṭamoḻiyi viṭa ivvakiyil iṉṟiya vaṭa intiya uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷē vilakkiṉṟi muṟṟilum ivvoḻuṅkip piṉ paṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi urinaṭiyilkūṭa oṉṟiraṇṭiṭaṅkaḷil iv voḻuṅkukku māṟupāṭu ēṟpaṭukiṟateṉpati eṭkiṉsē eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār. āyiṉum potuppaṭa urinaṭiyil iv voḻuṅkuppaṭi viṉimuṟṟē īṟṟilum, taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ muṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ik koḷki nām ēṟṟukkoḷḷattakka uṟutippāṭu uṭiyataṉṟu; ēṉeṉil col oḻuṅku oṉṟiyēpaṟṟi vakippaṭuttuvatāyiṉ kirēkka moḻiyiviṭa ilattīṉum, āṅkilattiviṭa jermaṉum turāṉiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭi varum.





↑ vaṭamoḻiyilum, intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷilum orumikkum, paṉmikkum ovvoru vēṟṟumiyilum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ uḷa

↑ ”China's Place in Philology"—Mr. Edkins





﻿﻿cu. tirāviṭamum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷum



tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu peṟappaṭṭaviyē, avaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟaṉavākak kāṇak kiṭakkum coṟkaḷ yātō oru paṇṭip pākata moḻiyiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavi eṉṟu koṇṭirunta koḷkikaḷ iraṇṭum piḻipaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, aṟiñar cilar ivaṟṟiṟku nērmāṟāṉa koḷki yoṉṟik kūṟa muṉvantaṉar. vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu kiḷitta vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇappeṟum piṟa moḻik kalapput tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl ēṟpaṭṭatutāṉ eṉpatu ip putuk koḷki. ik koḷkiyip parappiyavarkaḷil taliyāṉōr pampāyic cārnta revareṇṭ ṭākṭar sṭīveṉcaṉ[1] eṉpavarum, nēpāḷattic cārnta hāṭjsaṉ[2] eṉpavarumē. ivarkaḷ karuttu: (1) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iyaṟkit tākku talkaḷālum, kāla māṟupāṭṭālum vaṭamoḻiyi liruntu kiḷittaviyalla; āṉāl vaṭamoḻi yallāta piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ āṟṟal mikka kalappiṉāl citivuṟṟaṉavēyām. (2) ip piṟamoḻikaḷ tirāviṭap pēccuṭaṉ oppumi yuṭiyaṉa; it tirāviṭap pēccē, paṇṭi niṣātarkaḷ pēccum, piṟa paḻaṅkālak kuṭikaḷiṉ pēccumākum eṉpatē.

ivviru pakutikaḷuḷ muṉṉiyatu piṉṉēyativiṭa uṟutippāṭuṭiyatākum; āṉāl atuvum oruvāṟu mikipaṭak kūṟappaṭṭateṉṟum, ataṉit tiruttiyamikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉiṟ kūṟutum: mutaṉmiyāṉa cilavaṟṟi nōkka vaṭamoḻi hintiyākavum, vaṅkāḷamākavun tiripuṟṟatu iyaṟki māṟutalē eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷatu. airōppāvil ilattīṉi liruntu eppaṭi ittāliyamum, spāṉiṣū-m kiḷit taṉavō, appaṭiyētāṉ iviyum. āṉāl, vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa amippōṭum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷōṭum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiyum paṇpuc ciṟappukkaḷiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumikaḷ āriyac cārpaṟṟa tākkutalkaḷiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu oruvāṟu teḷiyalām.

atilum, palvēṟupaṭṭa vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷu ḷellām orēpaṭittāṉa ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumikaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟami koṇṭu, itaṟkuk kāraṇam potuppaṭṭa oru tākkutalākavē irukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu eḷitil ūkikkappaṭum. ”nākarika kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaṅ kālac cuvaṭukaḷākiya paṇṭiya, cemmippaṭāta muraṭṭu moḻiccoṟkaḷē” itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu pērāciriyar uvilsaṉ kūṟukiṟār. potuvākavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ ittakiya tākkutal pattil orupaṅkum, marātti moḻiyil maṭṭum aintil orupaṅkuntāṉ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷamiyāl, āriyac cārpaṟṟa kalappē kāraṇam eṉpatu naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.

vaṭamoḻi pēciya pārppaṉarkaḷ, ṣattiriyarkaḷ, viciyarkaḷ eṉṟa vakuppiṉarka ḷaṭaṅkiya āriyarkaḷatu varukikku muṉṉē, vaṭa intiyāviṉ perum pakutiyil "tasyūkkaḷ, niṣātar, milēccar” eṉṟu avarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭu vanta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē vāḻntuvantirun taṉar eṉpatum, ammakkaḷ cittiya iṉattiyō, aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa vēṟōr iṉattiyō cērntavarkaḷ eṉpatum aṉivarālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭa uṇmikaḷākum. ip paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ pēciya tiruntāta cittiya moḻic coṟkaḷ, paṭiyeṭuttuvantu veṟṟipeṟṟa āriyarkaḷ tam colvaḷamum, poruṇmiyum niṟinta vaṭamoḻi veḷḷattil āḻntupōyviṭutal iyalpē. eṉiṉum, ap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻi orēyaṭiyāy aḻintoḻiyāmal, taṉṉi viḻuṅkavanta vaṭamoḻi yiyē uraṅkoṇṭu tākkik kalakkit taṉṉuṭiya coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟip pukuttip putiyatoru matukiyiyum naṭip pōkkiyum atu mēṟkoḷḷac ceytirukkavēṇṭu meṉpatu piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ūkikkappaṭum: (1) cittiya moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa amippu niliyāṉatum, etirppukku īṭukoṭukkum āṟṟalum vāyntatu. (2) āriyarkaḷi nōkka ap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ tokiyil mikuntavarkaḷā yiruntaṉar. (3) ataṉāl, avarkaḷi āriyarāl aṟavē aḻittoḻikka muṭiyavilli. (4) mutaṟkaṇ aṭimika ḷākkappaṭṭu nāḷāvaṭṭattil āriyak kūṭṭattiṉaruṭaṉē cērkkappaṭṭaṉar.

ik koḷki maṟṟek koḷkikaḷiyum viṭa iṉṟiya nilimiyit teḷivupaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ coltokutiyilmaṭṭumē vaṭa moḻic cārpu mikutiyāyiruppatum, ilakkaṇa amippu cittiya moḻic cārpuṭiyatā yiruppatum nōkka, am moḻikaḷ cittiyap pakutiyuṭaṉ kalanta āriya aṭippaṭi yuṭiyavi eṉpativiṭa, āriyattuṭaṉ kalanta cittiya aṭippaṭiyi uṭiya moḻikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē mikavum poruttamuṭiyatākum. im moḻikaḷuḷ “tāttāriya allatu cālṭiya” [3] (atāvatu cittiya)k kalappu uḷḷatu eṉa mutaṉmutal kūṟiyavar car. uvilliyam jōṉs[4] āvar. itaṉi maṟuppavaruḷ taliyāṉavar hinti moḻiyip paṟṟi, 1872 - ēpril iṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭikkuvari[5] eṉṟa veḷiyīṭṭil kaṭṭuri oṉṟeḻutiya kireḷas pi. si. es.[6] āvar. avar hintimoḻi oṉṟi maṭṭum ārāyntuḷḷār; atilum ilakkaṇa amippi viṭṭuc col tokuti oṉṟi maṭṭumē ārāyntuḷḷār. ākiyāl iṉṉum ittuṟiyil nuṇuki ārāyntu muṭipu kāṇum variyil, cittiya aṭippaṭi muṭipē nilavi varum.

iṉi, ṭākṭar sṭīveṉsaṉ koḷkiyiṉ piṉṉiya pakutiyi nōkkuvōm. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil vaṭa moḻi yallāta piṟa moḻik kalappu irukkiṟateṉpatu oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉ, ap piṟamoḻi tirāviṭa moḻi yiṉattic cērntatē eṉpatu ap pakutiyākum. ik koḷki avvaḷavu tiṭṭamāka ārāyntu niṟuvappaṭuva toṉṟaṉṟu. ik koḷkippaṭi (1) intiyāviluḷḷa moḻikaḷ (vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷāyiṉuñ cari) ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, cittiya moḻik kalappō, tirāviṭa moḻik kalappō perum pālum orē paṭittāṉatu. (2) āriyarāl mutalil kippaṟṟap peṟṟa vaṭa intiyak kōṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ ak kalappiṉ aḷavu mikavuṅ kuṟukiyatu. (3) caṟṟuc cēymiyiluḷḷa takkāṇam, teliṅkāṇam, micūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil ak kalappiṉ aḷavu caṟṟup perukiyatu. (4) nila mukkiṉ teṉkōṭiyil iruppatum, maṉuviṉ kālattilum, irāmāyaṇa kālattilum pārppaṉa ātikkam kārt tikip piṟipōl iruntuvarappeṟṟatumākiya tamiḻ nāṭṭilō atu mikap parantu perukiyiruntatu.

mukaṟkaṇ, immuṭipu paṇṭiya intiya varalāṟṟiṉ pōkkōṭu ottatākavē kāṇappaṭum. eṉiṉum, moḻi yārāyccik kaṇṇāṭikoṇṭu pārkkiṉ vaṭa intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum, teṉṉintiyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷum orē iṉattār eṉṟu colliviṭuvataṟkilli. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippu cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu ottiruppatāṉālum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuṭaṉ ciṟappāka ottiruppatākak kāṇappaṭavilli. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ kāṇap peṟum vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu evvaḷavu toṭarpuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatō, avvaḷaviṟkuk kīḻit turukkiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉō, aṉṟic cittiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉōṭu toṭar puṭiyatākavuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ātalāl mēṟkoḷki eḷitil oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatoṉṟāka illi.

vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiṟkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amippiṟkum uḷḷa talimiyāṉa oppumikaḷ kīḻvarupaviyām:–

(1) peyarccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ urupukaḷ cērntu vēṟṟumikaḷ uṇṭātal. (2) paṉmip peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumi orumiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭāmal paṉmi vikutiyi mutalil cērttup piṉ vēṟṟumi urupukaḷic cērppataṉāl uṇṭātal. (3) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pōlavē taṉmip paṉmiyil muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭutti oṉṟum, vilakki oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruttal. (4) urupukaḷum, oṭṭukkaḷum peyarukkumuṉ varāmal piṉvarutal. (5) viṉic colliṉ kālatti iṭinili mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ kāṭṭal. (6) taḻuvu vākkiyaṅkaḷ talimi vākkiyattiṉ muṉ varutal. (7) taḻuvum moḻi taḻuvappaṭum moḻikkup piṉ varutal.

vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇa amippil ittakiya oppumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyē. āṉāl ivaṟṟāl ivviraṇṭu moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷukkum nērāṉa uṟavu uḷa teṉak kūṟiviṭutaṟkilli. ēṉeṉil, iv voppumikaḷ tirāviṭamoḻikaḷōṭumaṭṭum ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumika ḷākāmal, cittiya iṉattic cērnta moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum, orē vakiyil, orē aḷavil kāṇappaṭum potuppaṭiyāṉa oppumika ḷākiṉṟaṉa. turukkiya maṅkōliya moḻikaḷukkum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭum vēṟṟumikaḷil yātoṉṟum, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭavilli. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, cittiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷilillāta peyareccatti eṭuttukkoḷvōm. itu kōṇṭu oṉṟu nīṅkalāka ēṉiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum uḷḷatu; āṉāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilum illi.

atip pōṉṟē, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ellā viṉittiripukaḷukkum oḻuṅkāka etirmaṟiviṉi irukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ etuvum vaṭintiya moḻikaḷil illi. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭappeyar eṇṇup peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟutāṉum avaṟṟuḷ illi. āṉāl, intiyāvukku appāl pehistaṉ paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum, cīṉa moḻiyilum, ostiyakku[7] moḻiyilum, lāppar[8] moḻiyiluṅkūṭa ivaṟṟuṭ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum, im moḻikaḷil tirāviṭat tākku etēṉum iruntirukkak kūṭumāyiṉ, ki, kāl, kaṇ, kātu (cevi) pōṉṟa mutaṉmiyāṉa cila coṟkaḷēṉum, avaṟṟiṉ citivukaḷēṉum ivaṟṟuḷ em moḻiyilāyiṉum kāṇappaṭāmalirukka iṭamilli. uṇmiyil attakiya ec collum ivaṟṟuḷ yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭa villi. pērāciriyar sṭīveṉsaṉ kaṇṭa cila oppumikaḷ aruvaḻakkāy eṅkō mūliyilirun teṭukkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ ākum. vēṟu mutaṟcoṟkaḷum, ilakkaṇa amiti oppumikaḷum illāviṭattu ac ciṟupāṉmiyāṉa oppumikaḷik koṇṭu oru muṭivukku varamuṭiyātu. ākavē, mutaṉmiyāṉa vērc coṟkaḷip poṟutta variyil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, vaṭa intiya moḻi kaḷukkumiṭiyē ettuṇi vēṟupāṭu! āṉāl tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum ettuṇi oṟṟumi! viyakkattakka iv vuṇmikaḷik kīḻvarum aṭṭavaṇiyiṟ kāṇalām: taṉmi muṉṉili yorumip peyar, iṭap peyarkaḷuḷ eḻuvāyil or uruvum, piṟa vēṟṟumikaḷil vēṟu uruvum, viṉivikutikaḷil maṟṟōruruvumāka māṟi varuvatuṭaṉ, orē iṭattilum urukkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil oruvakiyākavum, piṟkālattil vēṟākavum irukkiṉṟamiyāl vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil avi yāvum oppumikkākat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

(piṉvarum aṭṭavaṉikaḷil eḷitil oppiṭṭaṟitaṟkāka vaṭamoḻi iṭappeyarkaḷum, turukkiya iṭappeyarkaḷum piṟikkuṟikaḷiṭṭu mutaliṟ koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.)

keḷara moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ iṭap peyarkaḷukkum malikkum maṭuvukkumuḷḷa vēṟṟumi yiruppati nōkka, 'am moḻikaḷiṉ tiripukku mūlakāraṇamāṉa tākkutalkaḷ vēṟeviyāyiṉum ākakkūṭum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ākā eṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, atē iṭap peyarkaḷiṉ ārāycciyālēyē pala vaṭamoḻikaḷilum cittiya vikutiyākiya ṉakaram iruppatu kāṇalām. ataṉōṭu, taṉmiyil vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟilēṉum vaṭamoḻi eḻuvāyuru illāmal, turukkiya moḻiyiṉ eḻuvāyip pōṉṟa mi(ṉ) allatu ma(ṉ) eṉṟa cittiya tōṟṟamuṭiya uruvē nilavutal kāṇka. oruvēḷi etirkāla ārāyccikaḷāl tirāviṭa uṟavu uṇṭeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭālum ēṟpaṭalām. eṉavē, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭa moḻic cārpaṟṟa pakutiyum, teṉ intiya moḻikaḷum iraṇṭum āriyac cārpaṟṟavi eṉpatum, iraṇṭum cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉpatun tavira vēṟu evvakiyilum oppumiyuṭiyaṉa alla eṉṟē tuṇiyat takum.

iṉi, ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil maṭṭumalla, vaṭa moḻiyilēkūṭa, tirāviṭat tākkuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉkiṟār.[1] inti moḻiyi ārāynta kireḷas eṉpavarō, vaṭamoḻic cārpaṟṟa pakuti yeṉṟoṉṟu avvaḷavā yiruntirukka vaḻiyilli eṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟkuk kīḻvarum kāraṇaṅkaḷik kāṭṭukiṟār: (1) ammoḻiyiṉ paḻamiyāṉa ilakkaṇa nūlār ataṉik kuṟippiṭātatu. (2) vaṭa moḻic cārpaṟṟateṉa virintu tuṇiyappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ, nuṇṇiya ārāycciyāl, vaṭa

* * *



↑ ТҺе Journal of the German Oriental Society for 1869.





moḻi yeṉṟu perumpālum niṟuvappaṭṭu varukiṉṟami. (3) vaṭamoḻi vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil, piṟamoḻit tākkutalkaḷāl vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tiripuṟṟamikku eṉṉeṉṉa niyatikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉavō anniyatikaḷi ikkāla vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷum piṉpaṟṟivarukiṉṟami. ic ceytiyik kuṟittu 1872-l pīms eṉpār ”āriyac cārpaṟṟa ik kāla intiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam”[1] eṉṟa nūlil piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār: ”iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyumallāta āriyamumallāta coṟkaḷip paṟṟi ārāyvōm. āriyar intiyāvukku vantapōtu tamakkumuṉ aṅkuk kuṭiyirunta makkaḷōṭu neṭunāḷ pōriṭṭu nāṭṭi avarkaḷiṭamiruntu paṟittāka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. attakiya pōrkaḷukkiṭiyē amiti nilaviya kālamum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. appōtu iru tiṟattiṉarum kalantu oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar karuvip pōr ceyyātu, vāḻkkip pōr naṭāttiyiruppar. āriyāllātā riṭiyē āriyaratu tākkē mikutiyāyirun tiruppiṉum, āriyariṭiyē āriyarallātār tākkum oraḷaviṟku illātirukka muṭiyātu. ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyilēyē āriyac cārpaṟṟaṉavākat tōṉṟum coṟkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟami iyalpē. ik kāla vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷilō ic coṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ec coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākat tōṟṟavilliyō aviyellām āriyac cārpaṟṟaviyē eṉṟu kūṟa muṉvaruvatu vaḻakkamākiviṭṭatu.

“iṅṅaṉam āriyarkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ vāṅkiṉar eṉṟa koḷkiyi oppukkoḷvataṟkut

1.

taṭikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. moḻiyiyaliṟ piṟa cāṉṟukaḷiṉ tuṇiyillāviṭattu veṟum colloppumi koṇṭu etiyum tuṇintuviṭa muṭiyātu. pala vaḻikaḷilum ciṟanta pōṟiñarkūṭa it tuṟiyil, ”āṉikkum nāṉku kāl, pūṉikkum nāṉku kāl; āṉik kālilum pūṉik kālilum nakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa oppumikaḷiṉ pēril muṭivukaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟaṉar. oru tamiḻc collil oru pakaram irukkiṟatu; oru vaṭacollilum oru pakaram irukkiṟatu. ākavē, vaṭacol tamiḻc colliliruntu vantatu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭuvatā! mutalāvatāka, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiviṭa uṭalvaliyilum aṟivuvaṉmiyilum āriyarkaḷ uyarvuṭiyavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ēṟṟativiṭa īntatē mikutiyā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum. iraṇṭāvatāka, intiyāvukku varuvataṉ muṉṉarē āriyarkaḷ colvaḷa mikkatoru moḻiyi uṭiyavarāyiruntaṉar. eṉavē uṭi, karuvikaḷ, taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷ, kaṉṟu kālikaḷ, uṭaṟ kūṟukaḷ, uṟavu muṟikaḷ pōṉṟa potuk karuttukkaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷikkūṭa avarkaḷ kaṭaṉvāṅka vēṇṭum nilimi avarkaḷiṭam iruntirukkamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ kaṭaṉ vāṅkavēṇṭu meṉṟāl putiya ceṭi koṭikaḷ, uyir vakikaḷ, tām muṉṉark kaṇṭirāta putup poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷiyēyākum. ivaṟṟilkūṭat tam moḻic coṟkaḷiyē tirittum kūṭṭiyum putuc coṟkaḷi ākkik koḷḷum āṟṟal ellā moḻikaḷukkum potuvāy amintuḷḷatu. mūṉṟāvatāka, niliyalip[2] poṟuttu eḻum cikkalkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āriyar varukiyiṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ naṭpākavō pakiyākavō uṟavupūṇṭa makkaḷ yār yār? vantēṟiya āriyarka ḷel lōrumē tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅki uṟavāṭi irukka muṭiyumā? muṭiyumeṉṟāl eppoḻutu? eppaṭi? iṉṟiya ārāycci niliyil iv viṉākkaḷukkut tiṟampaṭa viṭiyiṟuppatu aritu. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟiṟku etāvatu oru vaḻi kāṇal vēṇṭum. kaṅkik kariyiṉiṉṟum āriyarāl teṟkē viraṭṭappaṭṭa makkaḷ kōlēriyarum, vaṭakkē viraṭṭappaṭṭavar tipēttuk kalappumakkaḷum āvar. eṉavē, kōlēriyar moḻic cāṉṟiṉṟi vaṭa moḻic col oṉṟu nērākat tamiḻiliruntu peṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpati oppuk koḷḷutal cālātu. ittakiya nilimiyil ac colliṉ mūlatti ārāyum pakuti uṇmiyil vaṭamoḻiyum, ataṉ kiḷimoḻikaḷum eṉṟa aḷavukkē kuṟukiviṭu meṉpataṟkiyamilli. āḻntu nōkkiṉāl atil perumpālum ataṟkuriya vērccollik kāṇalām.”

ik kūṟṟu oruvāṟu oppukkoḷḷak kūṭiyatēyām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip paṟṟiyavari vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ tākkutal oruciṟitutāṉ iruntirukkak kūṭum. āṉāl, cittiyat tākkutalkaḷālō aṉṟi āriyac cārpaṟṟa piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷālō, vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ iṉṟiya tirinta niliyi aṭintirukkak kūṭāvō eṉpatu vēṟu kēḷvi. tirāviṭam, cittiyam, āriyac cārpaṟṟavi eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ kiḷavikaḷalla. pīms eṉpār ’uyir olimāṟṟaṅkaḷ'[3] eṉṟa pirivil, ”āriya uyirolikaḷ intiyāvukku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum naṭuvilum teṟkilum uḷḷa veḷi iṉattārāl evvaḷavu māṟutalaṭintaṉaveṉṟu collamuṭiyātu. āṉāl, māṟutal aṭintuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu maṭṭum maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmiyākum” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷuḷ akaramum ākāramum ēkāramāka māṟiyami, ittakiya āriyac cārpaṟṟa māṟutal eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṟār. nāvaṭi aṇṇa meyyākiya ḷakarattip paṟṟik kūṟukiyil, atu rakara ṭakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ mayaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉṟum, atu naṭu intiyāviluḷḷa kōlarkaḷiṉ kuḻuvil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟum avar urikkiṉṟār. ik kōlarkaḷuṭaṉum tirāviṭarkaḷuṭaṉum neṭunāḷ uṟavu koṇṭirunta marāṭṭiya, oriyā makkaḷiṭi, inta ḷakaram mikutiyākap payilvatu viyappaṉṟu eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.

“cinti moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇam”[4] eṉṟa nūlil ṭākṭar erṉasṭu ṭrampu āriyac cārpaṟṟa allatu tāttāriya moḻiyiṉat tākkutalkaḷ vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa’’ eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṟār; muṉ nā aṇpal oliyākiya takara nakara skaraṅkaḷiṉiṭamāka, avi nāaṭi aṇṇa oliyākiya ṭakara ṇakara ṣakaraṅkaḷip peritum ēṟkiṉṟami ataṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarum eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. atē pōṉṟu uyirppu oliyākiya ha eṉpatip pirākiruta moḻi vilakkuvatu nōkka, “atip pēciya potu makkaḷiṭiyē tāttāriyat tākku iruntirukkavēṇṭum. teṟkēyuḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil iv eḻuttu illāmi kāṇka” eṉṟum kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉattic cērnta teluṅku ‘cca’, ‘ja’ eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷi ‘ṭca’ eṉṟum, ṭs eṉṟum olippatu pōlavē marāṭṭi moḻiyum olikkiṉṟatu; ātalāl tirāviṭat tākku marāṭṭiyiluḷḷatu veḷippaṭi eṉṟum avar muṭivukaṭṭukiṟār.





↑ “A Comparative Grammar of the Modern Non-Aryan Languages of India" by John Beames B. C. S. 1872.

﻿e. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ enta iṉattic cērntavi?



﻿ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavē yaṉṟi intu-airōppiya moḻi yiṉattōṭu cērttaṟkuriyaṉavākā eṉpatu pērāciriyar rāsk[1] eṉpāriṉ muṭipākum. cērttaṟkuriyaṉa eṉṟu maṭṭum kūṟalāmē yaṉṟi, cērntaṉa eṉṟu colliviṭu taṟkilli; avi cittiya moḻikaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉa vēyaṉṟic cittiya moḻiyiṉattiliruntu peṟappaṭṭavi yalla. cittiya moḻiyiṉam eṉpatil piṉṉiṣ, turukki, maṅkōliyam, tuṅkūcasiyam ākiya moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. rāsk eṉpārē ivvāṟu mutaṉmutal vakippaṭuttiṉār. im moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē ilakkaṇa amippuṭi yaviyāy, orēpaṭittāṉa potu vitikaḷiyum piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. urupukaḷ allatu tuṇic coṟkaḷic cērppataṉālēyē avi ilakkaṇa vēṟupā ṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cemittiya[2] moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷilō pakutiyiṉ uyirolikaḷi māṟṟuvatāl vēṟupā ṭuṟukiṉṟaṉa; cīṉattilum, piṟa taṉippaṭṭa ōraci moḻikaḷilum coṟṟōṭariluḷḷa coṟkaḷi muṉṉum piṉṉum māṟṟi yamippatāl vēṟupāṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷum cittiya moḻikaḷip pōlavē mutaṉmutal, aṭukkiyal[3] muṟiyiyē kiyāṇṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl avaṟṟiṉ aṭikaḷ allatu tuṇic coṟkaḷ varavarat tēyntu vikutikaḷākavō urupukaḷākavō māṟiviṭṭamiyāl avi cittiya iṉaṅ kaḷip pōlavumiṉṟi, cīṉa moḻikaḷip pōlavumiṉṟi taṉippaṭṭa oruvaki iṉamāyiṉa. cittiya moḻikaḷic cilar tāttār moḻiyiṉam eṉpar; piṉṉiṣ iṉam, āltāyik iṉam, maṅkōliya iṉam, turāṉiya iṉam eṉṟum palavāṟākap palar kūṟuvar. āṉāl iviyaṉittum ip peru moḻik kuḻuviluḷḷa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭiyē taṉittaṉikkuṟikkumātalāl avaṟṟip peru moḻik kuḻuviṉ peyarākak koḷḷal cālātu. itukāṟum, paṇṭik kirēkka aṟiñarkaḷāl cittiyam eṉṟa peyar potuppaṭa āciyāvilum, airōppāvilum iruntuvanta nākarikamaṟṟa paṇṭi makkaḷik kuṟikkavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟuvantuḷḷamiyāl ataṉip peyarākavē vaḻaṅkutalē cālpuṭittām.

pēraṟiñar rāsk tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉak kūṟiṉavarkaḷuḷ mutalvarāyiṉum, ataṉit teḷivupaṭutti viḷakka ettakiya muyaṟciyum avar ceyyavilli. avarip piṉpaṟṟiya palarum atēmātiri avar kūṟiyatik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum ceyya villi. piriccārṭu[4] eṉpār tam ārāyccikaḷil cittiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ cila potuppaṭiyāṉa oṟṟumikaḷi oṉṟiraṇṭu ilakkaṇac cārpāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ, viḷakkip pōyiṉar. ātāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ āyntu viḷakkuvatu eḷitaṉṟu. ēṉeṉil aṅṅaṉam viḷakka vēṇṭumāyiṉ mutaṉ mutalil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷit taṉittaṉi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ oppumiyiṉmūlam avaṟṟiṉ paṇṭiya urukkaḷiyum ilakkaṇa amitikaḷiyum talimiyāṉa paṇpukaḷiyum aṟivatōṭu, atēmātirip piṟa cittiya uṭpirivukaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷiyum uṇarntāka vēṇṭum. ulaka varalāṟṟiṉ potuppaṭiyāṉa amitikaḷ”eṉṟa tokutiyil māksmūlaratu turaṉiyamoḻikaḷ paṟṟiya namatu iṉṟiya aṟivunili” eṉṟa ārāycciyuri piṉṉar veḷivantatu. annūlmikac ciṟantatoṉṟu. eṉiṉum, annūlil āciriyar tām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷ muḻuvatiyum, potuppaṭavē ārāyntuḷḷār. ataṉāl īṇṭu eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku atu putuccāṉṟukaḷ oṉṟiyum pakaravilli. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭar muṟiyum mēmpōkkākap pārppavarkkuk kūṭa viḷaṅkak kūṭumāyiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ olimuṟi, avaṟṟiṉ iṭavaki māṟṟaṅkaḷ, vaḻakkukaḷ, ilakkaṇa urumāṟṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi taṉippaṭṭa ārāycciyālaṉṟi viḷaṅkat takkaviyalla.

iṉi, ivvaki ārāycciyit toṭaṅkumuṉ pōṭliṅ eṉpār variyaṟuttuk kūṟiya, piṉvarum karuttu niṉivu kūraṟpāṟṟu : “ tām naṉku ārāyntaṟiyāta moḻikaḷippaṟṟi oruvar eḻuta muṟpaṭutal tīṅku viḷippatākum. cittiya moḻiyippaṟṟi ārāyavēṇṭumāṉāl, avaṟṟuḷḷaṭaṅkiya palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyalkaḷum vēṟṟumiyiyalkaḷum naṉku terintiruttal vēṇṭum. pālar’ eṉpār eḻutiya piṉṉiya moḻiyārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷum, pērāciriyar haṉḵpalvi' eṉpār eḻutiya turāṉiya moḻiyārāyccikaḷum ittuṟiyil pērutavi puriya vallaṉavām. haṅkēriyaṉ, vōkul" oskiyāk, piṉṉiṣ ākiya nāṉku moḻikaḷum orē potu moḻiyiṉattic cērntavi eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ

1. “On the present state of our knowledge of the Turanian Languages” by Max Muller in Bunsen's “Outlines of the Philosophy of Üniversal History. 2. Bohtlingh. 3. Bollar. 4. Prof. Hunfalvy.5. Vogul. ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa eṉṟum haṉḵpalvi viḷakkiyuḷḷār.

pēraṟiñar pāṭ, piraṭerik mullar[5] mutaliya pala moḻiyiyalār cittiya allatu turāṉiya moḻikaḷ oru potu moḻiyiliruntu vantavi eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. pēraṟiñar piḷākkum[6] turāṉiya moḻikaḷ palavum orupuṭi orē paṭittāṉa vaḷarcciyi aṭintuḷḷa orē kuḻu moḻikaḷō allatu pala kuḻu moḻikaḷō ākum” eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum potuppaṭa nōkka, kastrēṉ[7] mutaliya aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumikaḷ moḻikku iṉṟiyamiyātaviyākavum, palavākavum irukkiṟapaṭiyāl avaṟṟuḷ ēṟpaṭum toṭakkap potumiyi, aḵtāvatu orē mutaṉmoḻiyiṉiṉṟu avi toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṭivi, ēṟkātirukka muṭiyātu. immoḻikaḷiṉ col oṟṟumikaḷum vaṭiva oṟṟumikaḷum āriya cemikkiya iṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi yāyiṉum, taṟceyalāy ēṟpaṭak kūṭiyavi alla, orē mutaṉmoḻiyiliruntu ēṟpaṭṭaviyāvē irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivu” eṉpar aṟiñar māksmūlarum, mēlum neṭunāḷ pirintiyaṅkiya aṭukkiyal moḻikaḷuḷ, moḻiyiyaṟkiyiṉ aṭippaṭip pakutikaḷ "allatu māṟupāṭu ceyyamuṭiyāta col vakikaḷāṉa iṭappeyarkaḷ, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ, urupukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟit tavira vēṟu ettakiya oṟṟumikaḷiyum nām etirpārkka muṭiyātu. mēṟpaṭiyāṉa poruḷ koṇṭa coṟkaḷuḷ vēṟṟumi iruppativiṭa, oṟ ṟumi yiruppatik tāṉ nām arumiyāyk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟum avar kūṟukiṟār.

mēlē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭavi eṉpataṟkuk kāṭṭiya akkāraṇaṅkaḷiyē avi cikkiya iṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭavi eṉpataṟkum kāṭṭalākum. cikkiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷ aṉittum orē iṉam eṉpatiyum ak kāraṇaṅkaḷē kāṭṭum. avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkattik kuṟinta aḷavil eṭuttuk koṇṭāluṅkūṭa avi toṭarpu eṉpatu aiyamaṟat teḷivākum. cila kūṟukaḷil maṭṭum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷi oṭ ṭiyaviyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aḵtāvatu oraḷavukku, ' aṭukkiyal moḻiyāy iruppatiliruntu muṉṉēṟi (intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷip pōla) c citiviyal' moḻikaḷ ākiyuḷḷaṉa. vēṟṟumiyilum viṉittiripilum payaṉpaṭum tuṇic coṟkaḷiṟ pala taṉi moḻiyāka vaḻakkāṟaṟṟuppōyiṉa. āṉal iḵtoṉṟi muṉṉiṭṭu, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiya iṉattiliruntu pirikka vēṇṭiyatilli; ēṉeṉil ittuṇiccoṟkaḷ ciṟcila iṭaṅkaḷil muṟṟilum urupukaḷ allatu vikutikaḷākak kuṟintuviṭṭaṉavāyiṉum, coṟkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷiliruntu ivi eppoḻutum pirikkappaṭak kūṭiyaṉavākavē iruntuvarukiṉṟaṉa; intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷiṟ pōṉṟu ivi pakutiyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭu orē moḻiyiṉ kūṟāyviṭavilli. intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ivi aṅṅaṉam kalantu viṭṭapaṭiyāl pakuti etu, vikuti etu eṉṟu pirippatu kūṭa aritāy ṉaviṭukiṟatu. ataṉēṭu, turukkiyam, piṉṉiyam, haṅkēri

cittiya iṉaccārpuk koḷkiyi uṟutippaṭuttum vakiyil maṟṟoru cāṉṟuṅ kiṭittuḷḷatu. atuvē mēṟku mīṭiyā allatu pārasīkattil uḷḷa pehistaṉ allatu pakistāṉ aṭṭavaṇikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukkaḷ ākum. iv aṭṭavaṇikaḷ ṭarayas hisṭāspas" eṉpavar tammippaṟṟik tāmē eḻutiya varalāṟu ākum. ivi paḻam pāracīka moḻiyilum, pāpilōṉiya moḻiyilum, pārasīka, mīṭiyap pēraraciluḷḷa cittiya makkaḷum aṟiyum paṭiyāka avarkaḷatu tāymoḻiyilum eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ moḻipeyarppu tirāviṭa cittiya uṟavi viḷakkum vakiyil oru puttoḷiyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām tokuti teḷivākac cittiyac cārpāṉatu eṉṟu nāris’ tamatu kaṭṭuriyil eḻutukiṟār. pēraṟiñar ālparṭ immoḻiyip pēciya makkaḷ mīṭiyarē eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. āyiṉum avarum immoḻi cittiya allatu turāṉiyac cārpāṉateṉṟē koḷkiṟār. itaṉāl, ki. mu.5-m nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē pēcappaṭṭa oru cikkiya iṉa moḻiyuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷi oṟṟumippaṭuttum or ariya vāyppuk kiṭittuḷḷatu. immoḻi potuppaṭac cittiya iṉaccārpuṭiyatē. eṉiṉum, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟappāka ukrōpiṉṉiṣ kuḻuvuṭaṉ iṉṉum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatākavuṅ kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. tirāviṭamoḻi

Prof. Oppert. kaḷ inta, ukrō - piṉṉiṣ moḻikaḷic ciṟappāka ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu iṅkē kūṟavēṇṭiyatoṉṟākum.tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, ip pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ moḻikkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭum oṟṟumikaḷ varumāṟu : ---

(i) iv aṭṭavaṇikaḷil ṭ ṭa, ṭa, ṇa eṉṟa nā aṭi aṇṇa olikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. itē olikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyilum ivi uḷḷaṉavāyiṉum (piṟa āriya moḻikaḷuḷ illāmiyi nōkkak) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi vaṭamoḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa olikaḷē ivi eṉa ūkikkalām. nārisum ikkaruttiyē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.

(2) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻippōla, iv aṭṭavaṇi moḻikaḷuḷ valleḻuttukkaḷ moḻimutalil varumpoḻutum iraṭṭitta iṭattum valloli uṭiyaviyākavum, moḻi iṭiyē uyirkaḷiṉ naṭuvē varum poḻutu aṇmi allatu ari aṅkāppu oliyuṭiya ṉavākavum olikkiṉṟaṉa.

(3) ivvaṭṭavaṇi moḻiyiṉ āṟum vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ ṉa, ṉiṉa, iṉṉa eṉpaviyākum. ivaṟṟiṟkuc cariyāṉa tirāviṭa urupukaḷ teluṅku ṉī eṉpatum, kōṇṭu allatu pirākuvi ṉa a eṉpaviyum, tamiḻ iṉ eṉpatum ākum.

(4) aṭṭavaṇikaḷil nāṉkām vēṟṟumi urupu ikki allatu ikka eṉpatākum. itaṉiyokka urupukaḷ tāttār-turukkiyak kuḻuvilum, ukriyaṉ iṉattilum uḷa. āṉāl itaṉi muṟṟilum ottiruppavi tirāviṭa urupukaḷākiya ku, ki, ka eṉpaviyām. tamiḻ, maliyāḷa moḻikaḷil ivvurupukaḷ iraṭṭikkumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ muṉ u allatu i collolic cāriyiyāka varukiṉṟatu. aṭṭavaṇikaḷil uṉakku eṉṉum poruḷil niṉikka(niṉ -ikka) eṉṉum colluṭaṉ kaṉṉaṭam niṉake (niṉ-a-ke) maliyāḷam niṉakku (niṉ-a-kku) eṉpavaṟṟi oppiṭṭu nōkkuka.

(5) aṭṭavaṇikaḷiluḷḷa iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupu uṉ, iṉ, ṉ eṉpaviyām. ittuṭaṉ teluṅku urupu ṉu, ṉi eṉpatiyum, kaṉṉaṭam am, aṉṉu (aṉṉ-u) eṉpatiyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuka. (6) aṭṭavaṇikaḷil varum eṇṇup peyarkaḷil eḻuttil eḻutiya peyar oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷil varum kir eṉṟa col maṭṭumēyām. iḵtu uṇmiyil peyaruḷiccol vaṭivattilēyē (aḵtāvatu paṇput tokiyākavē) eḻutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. teluṅkil ivvaṭivam oka-eṉpatum, tamiḻil or-eṉpatum ākum. ku-moḻiyil tamiḻ oru eṉpataṟkuc cariyāṉa col ra eṉpatām. ik ku moḻiyiṉ vaṭivattiṟku aṭṭavaṇiyiluḷḷa piṟitoru vaṭivamākiya ra allatu irra mikavum neruṅkiyatākum.

aṭṭavaṇiyil eṇṇup peyarkaḷ peyaraṭiyākum poḻutu im eṉṟa cāriyiyik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. itu tamiḻ ām eṉpatiyum sāmōyiyat im eṉpatiyum niṉivūṭṭukiṉṟatu.

(7) tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum, aṭṭavaṇiyilum muṉṉilip peyar oṉṟē. ivi iraṇṭilum eḻuvāy nī; piṟavēṟṟumikku muṉṉiya mutal vaṭivu niṉ eṉpatākum. tamakkuk kīḻppaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷi aḻikka muṉṉili orumiyiyē vaḻaṅkivanta kāraṇattāl ivvaṭṭavaṇikaḷil muṉṉilippaṉmi kāṇappaṭavilli. (8) aṭṭavaṇi moḻiyilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip pōlavē peyareccam irukkiṟatu.āṉāl atōṭukūṭavē atil iṇippu iṭappeyar oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ākum. ippakuti pāracīkattiṉiṉṟum moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭatātalāl pāracīkattiluḷḷa iṇippu iṭappeyarittāṉ iṅkē puṉintu uṇṭupaṇṇiyirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu nāris karutukiṟār. aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ peyareccam uruvil tirāviṭap peyareccattiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum ataṉ poruḷum payaṉum tirāviṭa vaḻakki ottē irukkiṉṟaṉa. ippeyareccamē cikkiya iṉac cārpuṭiya moḻikaḷ aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉa ciṟappup paṇpu eṉak koḷḷalām pōlum!



(9) aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ ēval etirmaṟi muṭipu iṉṉi, kōṇṭil itu miṉṉi ākum.

ivvoṟṟumikaḷ nīṅkalākap piṟavēṟṟumit tiripukaḷil pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇiyiṉ moḻi haṅkēriyam, mōrṭuviṉ mutaliya piṟa ukriya moḻikaḷi ottirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, ivvakikaḷilellām tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ viṉiccollākkam cikkalaṟṟatu. avi perumpālum kālaṅkāṭṭum iṭinilikaḷuṭaṉum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ, pāl, iṭam kāṭṭa avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭappeyarkaḷiṉ tiripukaḷiyē koṇṭum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉ. ivvakiyil vēṟṟumikaḷ iruntumkūṭa, mēṟkūṟiya oṟṟumikaḷ iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappuṭiyaṉa ātaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum cittiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyiluḷḷa uṟavu oruciṟitēyāyiṉum aḵtu aṭippaṭiyāṉatē eṉpatil aiyamirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉil, ivvaḷavu vēṟṟumikaḷukkiṭiyilum iṉṟiyamiyāta oṟ

iṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇa amippu mutaliya pala oṟṟumip paṇpukaḷiṉ mūlam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiyak cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭiṉum, avi intu - airōppiya moḻiyiṉac cārpum uṭiyaviyē eṉpatu viyakkattakka cila oppumikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ cittiyat toṭarpukūṭat taṉippaṭat turukkiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉē, ukriya, maṅkōliya, tuṅkūsiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉē aṉṟu ; ammoḻikaḷ aṉittum tammuḷ evvaḷavō vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa ; āyiṉum aviyaṉittiṟkum, tāyāyuḷḷa oru pēriṉa moḻiyuṭaṉēyē tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉaṅkaḷ toṭarpuṭiyavi yām. ivaṟṟuḷ tirāviṭattiṟku mikavum aṇmiyuṭiyateṉak koḷḷakkūṭiyatu piṉṉiṣ allatu ukriyamē. oskiyakkuṭaṉum cila ciṟappāṉa uṟavukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ilakkaṇa oppumi,col tokuti oppumi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlamākamaṭṭum ārāyntu kaṇṭa immuṭipu pehistaṉ aṭṭavaṇikaḷiṉ veḷiyiṭṭāl peritum uṟutiyaṭivatu kāṇka. itaṉāl mīṭō-pāracīkaratu paṭiyeṭuppiṟku

muṉ naṭu āciyāvil kuṭiyirunta makkaḷ turukkiya allatu maṅkōliyak kuḻuviṉar allar, ukriyak kuḻuviṉarē eṉpatu viḷaṅkum. veppa maṇṭalattuḷ irukkum roviṭak kuḻu eṅkē, vaṭa turuvap pakkattilirukkum piṉṉiyar, oskiyakkar, ukriyar eṅkē! ivarkaḷiṭiyē ettikiya toṭarpu?

kirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiyac cārpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kaṇittaṟkāṉa teḷivukaḷi ārāykiyil niṉivil vikka vēṇṭiyatoṉṟuṇṭu. aḵtāvatu, orupuṟam cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa toṭakka iyal’ paṇpukaḷ teḷivāṉavuruvuṭiyavi āka iruppiṉum, iṉṉorupuṟam col tokutiyilum, vēṟu pala ciṟu kuṟippukkaḷilum oṉṟukkoṉṟu malikkum maṭuvukkum uḷḷa vēṟṟumi uṭiyaviyāyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatē. uṇmiyil, intu - airōppiya iṉa moḻikaḷ oṉṟukku oṉṟu vēṟṟumippaṭuvativiṭa, cittiya moḻikaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu ettaṉiyō maṭaṅku mikunta vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉittilum eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ ottirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉāṟ pōtātu, (vaṭamoḻi oṉṟu eṉṉum poruḷkoṇṭa eka eṉpatu nīṅkalāka maṟṟavi aṉittum) orē vaṭivuṭiyaviyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku nērmāṟāka, cittiya iṉa moḻikaḷil aṉittiṟkum potuvāṉa oṟṟumikaḷ arukiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu coṉṉum pōtātu; ētēṉum iraṇṭu moḻikaḷukkup potuvāṉa eṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷkūṭa mika māṟupaṭṭē yirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟuṅ kūṟutal vēṇṭum. ivvēṟṟumikaḷiṉ mikutippāṭṭi nōkkiṉ, intac cittiya iṉam intu - airōppiya iṉattip pōl oru taṉi iṉam eṉṟu colvativiṭap pala iṉaṅkaḷi uṭkoṇṭatōr pēriṉam eṉṟu colvatē cālum. ippēriṉattuṭpaṭṭa kuḻukkaḷiṉ orumippāṭu nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumikaḷāl niṟuvakkūṭiyataṉṟu, perumpōkkāṉa vaṭivu allatu amippu oṟṟumikaḷiṉālēyē niṟuvappaṭum. eṉavē, intu airōppiya iṉattuṭpaṭṭa moḻikaḷākiya vaṭamoḻi, sentu (kīḻap paḻam pārasīkam), paḻampārasīkam, kirēkkam ilattīṉam, kātik littuvēṉiyam, slavōṉiyam, kelttiyam mutaliya ovvoṉṟum ōriṉattuṭpaṭṭa taṉi moḻikaḷēyām. āṉāl cittiyap pēriṉattilē ivaṟṟilum mikunta vēṟṟumikaḷiyuṭiya ciṟṟiṉa moḻikaḷ aintāṟukku mēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvuṭpiriviṉaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟilum kiṭṭattaṭṭa intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷil ettaṉit taṉi moḻikaḷ uṇṭō attaṉit taṉi moḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivipōka vakippaṭutti innāḷ vari iṉamākac cērkka muṭiyāta niliyuṭiya irupatu muppatu taṉimoḻikaḷ kūṭa uḷḷaṉa.

intu - airōppiya iṉattiṟkum cittiyap pēriṉattiṟkum iṭiyil uḷḷa ipperu vēṟṟumikkuk kāraṇam mutaṉmutaṟ koṇṭē muṉṉiya iṉattavar uyar aṟivum nākarikamum paṭittiruntamiyum, avarkaḷ moḻikaḷ iṉṉum muntiya nāḷ mutaṟkoṇṭē tiruttamuṟṟu mutaṟ coṟkaḷum amitikaḷum citiyāmal pātukākkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷamiyum tāṉ. cittiya iṉattavar nāṭōṭikaḷāyt tiripavarāyiruntamiyum, āriyar oḻuṅkāṉa kuṭivāḻkki uṭiyavarāyiruntamiyum itaṟku aṭippaṭit tūṇṭutalkaḷāyiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kāraṇam etuvāyiṉum cari, cittiya moḻikaḷiṉ iṉa uṟavukaḷi ārāyum vakiyil icceytiyi niṉivil vittuk koḷḷal vēṇṭum. neṭunāḷ pirikkappaṭṭa cittiya iṉamoḻikaḷiṭiyē atē iyalpuṭiya intu - airōppiya - moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭum nuṇukkamāṉa oṟṟumikaḷi. etirpārkka muṭiyātu. tiruntā moḻikaḷiṭiyē, oru moḻiyiṉ vakittiripukaḷiṭikkūṭa mika virivāṉa māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭu, māṟupāṭum mikutiyāyviṭṭataṟkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷip pēraṟiñar māksmūlar tamatu "moḻiyiyal coṟpoḻivukaḷil” kuṟippiṭukiṟār. itē māksmūlarukku varinta kaṭitamoṉṟil pāṭṭisaṉ pātiriyārum ' oṉṟiṉiyoṉṟu aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷiraṇṭiṉ moḻikaḷ col tokutiyil muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭum, amippu oṉṟil maṭṭumē oṉṟupaṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatu iyalpu' eṉṟu itaṉēṭoppak kūṟutal kāṇka.

oru taṉimoḻikku oru moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa uṟavumuṟi irukka muṭiyum. (1) nērāṉa kālvaḻi muṟi (2) uṭaṉiyaṅkuṅ tuṇimoḻi muṟi (3) avviṉattuṭ cārum cārpumuṟi. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉrum uṟavu muṟiyiyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ cittiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavākak kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷ itaṉi oppukkoṇṭatākak kūṟa muṭiyātu. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ peritum intu-airōppiya iṉattic cērntaviyē eṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻiyārāyccip pēraṟiñar ṭākṭar pōp karutukiṟar. tamiḻk kic cuvaṭieṉṉum tam nūliṉ muṉṉuriyil avar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.

'teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷi evvaḷavukkevvaḷavu āḻntu paṭikkiṉṟōmō avvaḷavukkavvaḷavu avi vaṭamoḻit toṭarpuṭiyaṉaveṉavum, intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuṭaṉ mikavum neruṅkiya paṇṭit toṭarpu uṭiyaṉaveṉavum kāṇalām. āṉāl, avi veṟum pākataṅkaḷ eṉṟē, vaṭamoḻic citivukaḷ eṉṟē kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. avi intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattuḷ iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiyaṉavē eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻikku iṇiyāṉa vēṟoru tuṇimoḻiyiṉ citinta kūṟukaḷē avi eṉṟum, aviyum vaṭamoḻiyum orē paṇṭi mūla moḻiyiṉ kiḷikaḷ eṉṟum yām eppoḻutum karuti vantuḷōm. intu-airōppiya moḻiyiṉattic cērnta kirēkkam, kātik, persiyaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ avi neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa eṉpataṟku uṟutiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ pala uḷa ; attakiya toṭarpu vaṭamoḻikku iruntatākac colvataṟkilli”. Teutonic. kūṟiyatu: 'tutamoḻi ilakkaṇac curukkattiṉ muṉṉuriyil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār: pēraṟiñar kālṭuveluṭaṉ yām peritum otta karuttuṭiyōm āyiṉum, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum, kelttiya moḻikaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa uṟavu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyakkiṭappatoṉṟāl eṇṇukiṉṟōm’ eṉpatē. ittuṟi ārāycci mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatē eṉpatil aiyamilli. pōp intu - airōppiya moḻiyuṭaṉē oṟṟumi, uṟavu, iṉoṟṟumi eṉṟu kūṟuvaṉavellām emakku uṭaṉpāṭē. innūliṉ mutaṟpatippil avaṟṟi yāmē eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum uḷḷēm. āṉāl, av eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷiliruntu pōp koṇṭa muṭipu em muṭivāṉa muṭiyuṭaṉ vēṟupaṭukiṟatu. ematu kōṭpāṭu cittiya, intu-airōppiya uṟavukaḷ iraṇṭaṟkum iṭantarupaviyāka iruppiṉum, intu-airōppiya iṉattiviṭac cittiya iṉamē tirāviṭak kuḻuvōṭu neruṅkiya uyirniliyāṉa toṭarpu uṭiyatu eṉpatākum. kelttiya uṟavukaḷippaṟṟi eṭuttukkoṇṭāl, intu - airōppiya moḻikaḷ aṉittilum immoḻiyiṉamē potuppaṭac cittiya iṉattuṭaṉum, ciṟappāka piṉṉiyak kuḻuvuṭaṉum mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaviyākat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kelttiyar airōppāvukku varumpōtu aṅkirunta muṉ kuṭikaḷ kelttik moḻiyiyum piṉṉiṣ moḻiyiyum pēci vantārkaḷō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. mēlum, kelkkiya uṟavukaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uṟavukaḷ ciṟappākak kelttiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā allatu uṇmiyil cittiya uṟavukaḷ tāṉā eṉpatum oppiṭṭu ārāyattakkatoṉṟām.

1. Outline of the Grammar of the Tuda, Language. inta ārāycciyiṉ toṭakkattilēyē mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa kirāviṭa-intu-airōppiya oṟṟumikaḷ oppuk koḷḷappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ataṟkākak kuṟittuḷḷa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷil cila oṟṟumikaḷ aṭippaṭiyāṉavi alla; ātalāl āḻnta nōkkum aḷavil maṟintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. (tamiḻ oṉṟu allatu oṇṇu; ilattiṉam ūṉus; tamiḻ añcu ; vaṭamoḻi pañca(ṉ) ; tamiḻ eṭṭu ; vaṭamoḻi aṣṭa(ṉ) eṉpaṉa pōlpavi). ivaṟṟip puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭup pārttāluṅkūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amikikaḷiṟ palavum, mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ palavum intu-airōppiya moḻikaḷuṭaṉ oppumiyuṭiyaviyāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, uyirniliyāṉa amippu muḻuvatum aiyamaṟac cikkiyac cārpuṭiyatātalāl cittiya iṉa uṟavē valiyuṟuttappaṭṭatu. epirēya mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṟ pala vaṭamoḻi mutaṟcoṟkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭiyavi eṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟami karuki, epirēya moḻiyic cemitkiya iṉattiṉiṉṟum pirittu intu - airōppiya moḻiyākak kaṇikkātatupōla, vaṭamoḻi, kirēkkam, kākik, kelttik, pārasīkam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷuṭaṉ viyakkat takum oṟṟumikaḷ kirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum kirāviṭa moḻikaḷic cittiya iṉattiliruntum pirikka vēṇṭiyatilli. "kiṉikō. āryaka" eṉṟa nūlil[8] pēraṟiñar ṣlejel āriya cīṉa oppumikaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi āriya cīṉa moḻikaḷ tammuḷ iṉa uṟavuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu viḷakka muyalkiṟār. avar kūṟiya uṟavu uṟutippaṭṭuviṭiṉuṅkūṭac cīṉam āriyamoḻi eṉṟu āyviṭamāṭṭātu. iru moḻikaḷilum mikap paḻamiyāṉa cila mutaṟ coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟutāṉ muṭivu ceyyappaṭum. mēlum cīṉamoḻi maṭṭumē yaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻiyum, epirēyamuṅkūṭa mutaṉmutalil oraci moḻiyāy iruntaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu. perumpālum tirāviṭa mutaṟcoṟkaḷum aṅṅaṉam ōracic coṟkaḷē eṉpatu ivvārāycciyāl pulaṉākum. ivvakiyil pēraṟiñar pḷik eṉpār āriya moḻikaḷ mikap paḻimiyāṉatoru kālattil, tirāviṭa moḻi aḷiṉ kākkutalkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum” eṉak koḷkiṟār. avar kūṟuvatāvatu : -"pāl vakuppuṭiya moḻikaḷuḷ āriya iṉam potuppāl eṉa oru pāl uṭimiyāl vēṟu pirikkappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟiya āriya moḻikaḷuḷ ippotuppāl tiruntiya muṟiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu āṅkilam oṉṟilētāṉ. āṉāl, paḻiya moḻikaḷuḷ āṅkilattiṟku oppat tiruttamāka itaṉit tirāviṭa moḻik kuḻuvē vaḻaṅkivantuḷḷatu. āṅkilamum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷum ippāli vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷ, elli ākiyavaṟṟuḷ muṟṟilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. (āṇ, peṇ, potu eṉṟa) muppāl pākupaṭṭi uṭiya kuḻukkaḷ ivi iraṇṭumē eṉpati nōkka ivi uṟavaṟṟavi eṉṟu koḷḷutal iyalpākat tōṟṟavilli. pāl vakuppiyuṭiya āriya iṉattuṭaṉ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ itukāṟum toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭavilliyāyiṉum, āriya moḻikaḷ uru aṭiyāmal vaḷarntuvanta kālattil avaṟṟil tirāviṭattākku ēṟpaṭṭataṉālēyē ivvoṟṟumiyum piṟa oṟṟumikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu kūṟa iṭamuṇṭu” eṉpatē. ārāycci eṭṭumaḷavum pārttālum mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālattilkūṭat tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil ippotuppāl orumip (oṉ
